To evaluate the model's ability to generalize out of distribution, we design dynamical tasks under different gravitational fields that, despite obeying the same physical law, exhibit qualitatively different dynamics, ranging from floating motion in weak gravitational fields to rapid bouncing in strong ones. In contrast to DINO-WM, SG-JEPA reduces open-loop prediction error by up to 2 times on two-dimensional datasets, and increases control success rate up to 2.5 times for three-dimensional robotic datasets, for which we train independent diffusion policies. To explain this advantage, we develop a linear feature model that separates local law-conditioned error from its recursive amplification under rollout. Guided by this model, we find that back-propagating the multi-step rollout loss into the representation trains the encoder to keep the features that the predictor can carry forward, and that those are the features the dynamics depend on, so most of the gain comes from the encoder learning better features rather than from the predictor learning better dynamics. See project page at https://sg-jepa.github.io.

</details>


### [152] [Nonmaximal sums of maximally monotone operators under Rockafellar's constraint qualification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10487)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Rockafellar和猜想（在内部域条件下两个极大单调算子之和仍极大单调）的反例：分别在 $c_0$ 和 $\ell^1$ 上给出反例。核心工具是一个一般性构造定理，用于计算一类图的单调极集、给出极大单调性的充要条件，并通过正秩一扰动在满足该条件时得到非极大的和。作者用该定理验证了 $c_0$ 上的假设与极大性判别，并通过构造 $\ell^1$ 到 $c_0$ 的有界线性满射得到 $\ell^1$ 上的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rockafellar曾猜想：若两个极大单调算子满足内部域条件，则其和仍是极大单调的。这一猜想长期未决，且对单调算子理论及相关的凸分析、变分不等式有基础性意义。本文旨在通过构造反例来否证该猜想，并揭示内部域条件并不能保证和的极大单调性。

Method: 1) 建立一般性构造定理：针对一类图的单调极集给出完整计算公式，并导出其极大单调性的充要条件，同时说明如何通过正秩一扰动在该条件下产生非极大的和。2) 在 $c_0$ 空间中验证定理假设及极大性判别，从而得到第一个反例。3) 构造从 $\ell^1$ 到 $c_0$ 的有界线性满射，将 $c_0$ 上的构造转移，得到 $\ell^1$（配以通常范数）上的第二个反例。

Result: 成功构造了两个满足内部域条件但其和不再极大单调的极大单调算子对：一个在 $c_0$ 上，另一个在 $\ell^1$ 及其通常范数上，从而否证了Rockafellar和猜想。所得一般性构造定理给出了相关图类的单调极集显式表达、极大单调的充要条件，以及正秩一扰动导致非极大和的机制。

Conclusion: Rockafellar和猜想在内部域条件成立时并不普遍成立；内部域条件不足以保证两个极大单调算子之和的极大单调性。本文的构造定理和秩一扰动方法为研究单调算子的极大性提供了新的分析工具，并可在 $c_0$ 与 $\ell^1$ 等具体空间中系统性地生成反例。

Abstract: We construct counterexamples to Rockafellar's sum conjecture in which two maximally monotone operators satisfy the interior-domain condition but their sum is not maximally monotone. We give one counterexample on $c_0$ and another on $\ell^1$ with its usual norm. We establish a general construction theorem that computes the entire monotone polar of a class of graphs, gives a necessary and sufficient condition for their maximal monotonicity, and shows how a positive rank-one perturbation yields a nonmaximal sum under this condition. We verify the theorem's hypotheses and its maximality criterion on $c_0$, thereby obtaining a counterexample to the conjecture. Furthermore, we construct a bounded linear surjection from $\ell^1$ onto $c_0$ and use it to obtain the counterexample on $\ell^1$.

</details>


### [153] [Learning with Covariance Matrices: Principal Component Analysis Meets Learning with Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10490)
*Saurabh Sihag,Andrea Cavallo,Elvin Isufi,Gonzalo Mateos,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述协方差神经网络（VNN，即以协方差矩阵为图的图神经网络）的理论基础，涵盖其与PCA的概念等价性、协方差扰动下的预测稳定性界，以及跨多尺度数据集的迁移性刻画，并展示其在脑龄差估计等任务中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵在众多领域中普遍存在，现有GNN理论基于抽象图表示，无法刻画协方差矩阵所特有的数据驱动细节，因此需要专门针对VNN的理论分析来指导其设计与应用。

Method: 通过数学分析建立VNN的理论框架，具体包括：论证VNN与基于主成分分析（PCA）的信息处理之间的概念等价性；在有限样本导致协方差矩阵扰动的情形下推导更精细的预测结果稳定性界；刻画VNN跨多尺度数据集的迁移性。

Result: 获得了三方面的理论洞见：VNN与PCA处理的概念等价、协方差扰动下更紧的稳定性界、以及跨多尺度数据集迁移性的精细刻画，为在协方差矩阵作为数据结构描述符的场景中采用VNN而非传统PCA学习流程提供了原理性依据。

Conclusion: 这些基础理论进展推动了跨领域学习方法的原理性设计与应用，并具体体现在利用神经影像数据集刻画神经退行性疾病的脑龄差这一计算神经科学问题上，同时对其他应用领域具有广泛影响。

Abstract: This feature article provides an overview of the theoretical foundations for coVariance neural networks (VNNs), i.e., graph neural networks (GNNs) operating on covariance matrices as graphs. Covariance matrices are ubiquitous across domains, and hence, the deployment of GNNs often leverages graphs of pairwise statistical dependencies. Existing theoretical contributions on GNNs consider abstract graph representations and cannot accommodate the data-driven nuances associated with covariance matrices. This tutorial brings into focus various novel theoretical insights via mathematical analyses of VNNs that have broad signal processing implications, including: (i) a conceptual equivalence between VNNs and principal component analysis (PCA)-based information processing; (ii) refined stability bounds on predictive outcomes in the presence of finite sample-induced covariance matrix perturbations; and (iii) refined characterization of transferability of VNNs across multiscale datasets. The theoretical insights discussed herein provide the underlying principles and justification towards adopting VNNs over workhorse PCA-based learning pipelines, in applications where covariance matrices are useful descriptors of data structure. We also convey how impact of these foundational advances permeates to \textit{principled} designs and applications of learning methods across broad domains where covariance matrices emerge. Notably, we elucidate the conceptual insights facilitated by VNNs to the specific task of characterizing brain age gap for neurodegenerative conditions using neuroimaging datasets, a timely problem in computational neuroscience. Broader impacts to other application domains are discussed as well.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum Feature Engineering for Credit Default Prediction: When and Why IQP Circuits Help Linear Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10505)
*Menachem Finkelstein,Diana Legziel Levy,Zohar Yakhini,Sarel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在信用违约预测中，8量子比特IQP电路提取的16个特征可将逻辑回归的F1从0.462提升至0.517，优于核PCA等所有基线，机制为放大信息结构而非凭空创造信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用违约预测中F1的小幅提升可直接减少金融风险敞口。作者希望验证IQP量子电路能否在相同特征预算下，生成优于原始经典基线及最强无监督非线性方法Kernel PCA的特征。选择量子电路的动机是计算性的：n量子比特IQP电路为常数深度且编码2^n维希尔伯特空间中的特征关联，而其精确输出统计的经典模拟随n指数增长。

Method: 使用UCI Default of Credit Card Clients数据集与五折交叉验证。数据含23个财务属性；对n量子比特电路选取n个属性，各自编码为旋转角，读回2n个期望值作为新特征。将该特征追加到逻辑回归等分类器中（Random Forest、SVM、XGBoost、k-NN），并与Kernel PCA在相同特征数下对比；同时比较随机森林重要性引导选择与最大不相关特征选择的效果，并用Benjamini-Hochberg校正检验12项测试。

Result: 追加16个IQP特征（n=8）将逻辑回归F1从0.462提升至0.517（+0.055，p<0.0001）；Kernel PCA在同特征数下仅达0.493，差距在BH校正后仍显著（p=0.00007）。其他分类器均无获益，指向线性表达力机制。特征选择方式有影响：随机森林重要性引导选择达F1=0.523，而编码最大不相关特征降至0.496。

Conclusion: IQP电路特征在同等预算下显著优于经典基线，且获益特异于线性模型，说明其机制是放大数据中已有的信息结构而非从零创造信息；输入特征的选择策略对最终性能至关重要。

Abstract: Credit default prediction is a tabular classification problem in which modest gains in F1 translate directly into reduced financial exposure. We ask whether Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time (IQP) circuits can produce features that improve a classifier over both its raw classical baseline and Kernel PCA - the strongest unsupervised classical non-linear alternative - at an equal feature budget. The dataset provides 23 financial attributes per client; for an n-qubit circuit we select n of them, encode each as a rotation angle, and read 2n expectation values back out as new features. The motivation for using a quantum circuit is computational: an n-qubit IQP circuit runs in constant depth and encodes feature correlations in a 2^n-dimensional Hilbert space, whereas classical simulation of its exact output statistics scales exponentially in n. Using the UCI Default of Credit Card Clients dataset and five-fold cross-validation, we find that appending 16 IQP features (n = 8 qubits) to a Logistic Regression model raises F1 from 0.462 to 0.517 (+0.055, p < 0.0001). Kernel PCA, the next-best method, reaches only 0.493 at the same feature count; the gap survives Benjamini-Hochberg correction across 12 tests (p = 0.00007). No other classifier - Random Forest, SVM, XGBoost, or k-NN - benefits, which points to a linear-expressivity mechanism rather than a generic improvement. We also show that how the 8 input features are chosen matters: Random Forest importance-guided selection reaches F1 = 0.523, while encoding maximally uncorrelated features drops it to 0.496, demonstrating that the circuit amplifies informative structure rather than creating it from scratch.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [A Brunn--Minkowski inequality and Convexity for the 2-Hessian eigenvalue in convex domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09175)
*Jiahuan Li,Xi-Nan Ma,Guohuan Qiu,Paolo Salani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了2-Hessian方程正的第一特征函数的严格对数凹性及相应扭转问题解的严格1/2-凸性，并建立了Brunn-Minkowski不等式，同时通过四维反例表明3-Hessian方程不具有此类变换凸性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hessian方程解及其特征函数的凸性结构，推广经典Brunn-Minkowski理论到Hessian方程框架，并探讨变换凸性现象在更高阶Hessian方程中的适用边界。

Method: 在R^n中光滑有界一致凸区域上，对2-Hessian方程正的第一特征函数-u及相应扭转问题的解进行分析，证明严格对数凹性与严格1/2-凸性；并通过在四维空间构造光滑一致凸区域，其容许零边界解具有非凸次水平集，作为3-Hessian方程的反例。

Result: 证明了2-Hessian方程正的第一特征函数的严格对数凹性和扭转问题解的严格1/2-凸性，建立了相应的Brunn-Minkowski不等式；同时在四维中构造反例，证明3-Hessian方程不具备该变换凸性现象。

Conclusion: 2-Hessian方程具有变换凸性结构并可导出Brunn-Minkowski型不等式，但该现象在3-Hessian方程中失效，揭示了此类凸性现象对Hessian方程阶数的依赖性。

Abstract: We prove the strict log-concavity of the positive first eigenfunction \(-u\) of the \(2\)-Hessian equation and the strict $1/2$-convexity of the solution for the corresponding torsion problem in smooth bounded uniformly convex domains in $\mathbb{R}^{n}$. As applications, we establish the associated Brunn--Minkowski inequalities. We also show that this transformed-convexity phenomenon fails for \(3\)-Hessian equations by constructing, in dimension four, a smooth uniformly convex domain whose admissible zero-boundary solution has a nonconvex sublevel set.

</details>


### [156] [Breathing and moving vesicles in a geometric mechanochemical model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09360)
*Alexander Meiners,Hannes Uecker*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究囊泡的几何力学化学耦合模型，将Helfrich流与单一形态发生素的反应扩散方程耦合，通过数值延拓与分岔方法计算解分支的分岔图，主要针对平面囊泡（1D闭曲线），并展望3D情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Helfrich流是弯曲弹性能的L²梯度流，通常附加面积或体积约束；而将形态发生素动力学与膜形状耦合后，流不再具有梯度结构，从而可能产生更复杂的动力学行为（如时间周期轨道、"呼吸与移动"的囊泡形状）。作者希望刻画这类非梯度耦合系统的解分支与分岔结构。

Method: 建立几何力学化学模型：以Helfrich流描述脂双层膜形状X的演化（弹性能E(X)=∫_X (H-c_0)² dS的梯度流，可加面积/体积约束），并在X上引入单一形态发生素φ的反应扩散方程。φ在高/低平均曲率H处吸附/脱附（动力学依赖H），同时φ反过来修饰自发曲率c_0。采用数值延拓与分岔方法计算解分支的分岔图。

Result: 给出了计算该类耦合系统解分支分岔图的数值方法，适用于非梯度流情形，能够刻画包括时间周期轨道（"呼吸与移动"囊泡形状）在内的复杂动力学。主要结果针对平面囊泡（1D闭曲线），并给出3D囊泡（2D闭膜）的展望。

Conclusion: 形态发生素与膜形状的双向耦合使Helfrich流失去梯度结构，产生更丰富的动力学；所提数值延拓/分岔框架可有效计算其分岔图，为平面及3D囊泡的自组织形状动力学研究提供工具。

Abstract: We consider a geometric mechanochemical model of vesicles which couples the Helfrich flow for the shape of a lipid bilayer vesicle membrane $X$ with a reaction-diffusion equations for a single ``morphogen'' $φ$ on $X$. The Helfrich flow is the $L^2$ gradient flow of the elastic bending energy $E(X)=\int_X (H-c_0)^2 dS$ of $X$, typically supplemented by area or volume constraints, or both. The morphogen $φ$ adsorbs/desorbs at places of high/low mean curvature $H$, i.e., the kinetics of $φ$ depend on $H$, and conversely $φ$ modifies the spontaneous curvature $c_0$ on $X$. The flow is no longer gradient, and hence allows for more complicated dynamics, including time periodic orbits, e.g., ``breathing and moving'' vesicle shapes. We show how to compute bifurcation diagrams for such solution branches via numerical continuation and bifurcation methods. We mostly focus on ``planar'' vesicles (1D closed curves) but also give an outlook on 3D vesicles (2D closed membranes).

</details>


### [157] [The computational ansatz for convex integration of hyperbolic systems and a resolution of the Strong Trace Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09353)
*Sam G. Krupa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一维2×2双曲守恒律系统，利用计算辅助搜索找到满足稳定性与$a$-contraction条件的压力律，证明在强迹性质下Riemann解唯一，而无强迹性质时可构造非唯一解，从而刻画强迹的尖锐性，并排除$T_\infty$与$p''>0$时$T_N$构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前与Székelyhidi提出的计算ansatz表明，本构集的秩一凸几何与低正则解的存在性及非唯一性密切相关，因此需要研究该几何结构与稳定性的关系，特别是强迹性质对解唯一性的影响是否尖锐。

Method: 采用计算方法搜索，找到一个压力律$p$，使$p$-系统及其自然严格凸熵满足大初值$L^2$稳定性与带推移的$a$-contraction技巧所需全部条件；同时分析本构集的秩一凸几何，构造$T_6$构型；并对真正非线性系统证明$T_\infty$结构不存在，对$p''>0$的$p$-系统证明任意$N$的$T_N$结构不存在。

Result: 对所选压力律，在满足强迹性质的解类中得到某些Riemann解的唯一性；而本构集含$T_6$构型，可用于构造不满足强迹性质的非唯一解；证明了真正非线性系统不存在$T_\infty$结构，且$p''>0$时$p$-系统对所有$N$不存在$T_N$结构。

Conclusion: 结果解决了强迹性质的尖锐性问题：唯一性在强迹条件下成立，缺失该条件则非唯一；同时排除了通过$T_\infty$及$p''>0$时$T_N$构型进行凸积分的技术路线。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider $2\times2$ hyperbolic systems of conservation laws in one spatial dimension. We use the computational ansatz introduced in the hyperbolic theory by the author and Székelyhidi to study the constitutive set corresponding to the PDE. The rank-one convex geometry of this set relates to non-uniqueness and the existence of low-regularity solutions. Through a computer-assisted search, we find a pressure law $p$ such that the $p$-system with this pressure law, and its natural strictly convex entropy, verifies all of the conditions necessary for the large data $L^2$ stability and the technique of ``$a$-contraction with shifts'' and thus we have uniqueness of certain Riemann solutions in the class of solutions verifying the Strong Trace Property. At the same time, the constitutive set contains a $T_6$ configuration and we use it to construct non-unique solutions without the Strong Trace Property. This resolves the question of sharpness of strong traces. We also present proofs which show nonexistence of $T_\infty$ structures for all genuinely nonlinear systems and nonexistence of $T_N$ structures for all $N$ for the $p$-system with $p''>0$, thus blocking these routes towards convex integration.

</details>


### [158] [Global well-posedness for Vlasov equations with power-law interactions in $d\ge4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09512)
*Emmanouil Katriadakis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一般幂律（Riesz型）势Vlasov方程，证明维数d≥4、0<α<3范围内任意非负紧支撑有界初值的全局适定性，无需小性条件；吸引情形下α≥3负能解有限时间破裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带一般幂律（Riesz型）势的Vlasov方程，探究在高维（d≥4）及宽指数范围（0<α<3）内，是否对任意非负紧支撑有界初值（不施加小性条件）均成立全局适定性，并刻画吸引相互作用下解破裂的条件。

Method: 证明结合特征分析、Lagrangian坐标下的估计，以及Denis Serre提出的补偿可积性（compensated integrability）不等式的关键应用；对吸引问题利用virial恒等式分析负能解的有限时间破裂。

Result: 在d≥4、0<α<3范围内，对吸引与排斥相互作用均证明全局适定性，且初值仅为任意非负紧支撑有界函数，无需小性条件；对吸引问题，当α≥3时，virial恒等式给出负能解在有限时间内破裂。

Conclusion: 该工作将带一般幂律势Vlasov方程的全局适定性结果推广至高维d≥4及0<α<3的广大范围，覆盖吸引与排斥情形且不要求初值小；同时明确α≥3的吸引情形存在有限时间破裂，凸显补偿可积性不等式在处理此类问题中的核心作用。

Abstract: We study the Vlasov equation with general power-law (Riesz-type) potentials with exponent \(α\). We prove global well-posedness in every dimension \(d\ge4\), for both attractive and repulsive interactions, throughout the range \(0<α<3\), for arbitrary nonnegative compactly supported bounded initial data. No smallness condition is imposed on the initial data. For the attractive problem, the virial identity gives finite-time breakdown for negative-energy solutions when \(α\ge3\). The proof of global well-posedness combines the analysis of characteristics, estimates in Lagrangian coordinates, and a crucial application of a \emph{compensated integrability} inequality due to Denis Serre.

</details>


### [159] [Three-dimensional steady transonic shocks for the relativistic Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09522)
*Shangkun Weng,Yan Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究半球壳中稳态相对论Euler方程的跨声速激波问题，证明球对称解的存在唯一性，并在三维出口压力扰动下建立激波的存在性与稳定性，关键技术为利用形变张量与涡量解耦、球面投影坐标以及重述Rankine-Hugoniot条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态相对论Euler方程中的跨声速激波问题在激波前为超声速、激波后为亚声速，具有混合双曲-椭圆型特征，且球壳几何带来坐标奇异性，现有结果多限于背景激波受限的情形，缺乏对一般背景激波下三维扰动稳定性的研究。

Method: 采用打靶法并结合出口压力与激波位置之间的单调性，证明球对称跨声速激波解的存在唯一性；利用形变张量与涡量将稳态相对论Euler方程解耦为双曲与椭圆部分；引入结合球坐标与球极投影的"球面投影坐标"处理坐标奇异性；重述Rankine-Hugoniot条件以确定激波前沿，并为非局部形变-旋度系统导出激波前沿边界条件。

Result: 证明了球对称跨声速激波解的存在性与唯一性；在无背景跨声速激波限制的条件下，证明了出口压力三维扰动下跨声速激波的存在性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将相对论Euler方程跨声速激波理论推广至半球壳几何中的三维扰动情形，所发展的解耦方法、球面投影坐标与形变-旋度系统框架为处理混合型方程与坐标奇异性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We investigates the transonic shock problem for steady relativistic Euler equations in hemispherical shells. We show the existence and uniqueness of spherically symmetric transonic shock solutions via the shooting method and the monotonicity between the exit pressure and shock position. Furthermore, without any restrictions on background transonic shocks, we prove the existence and stability of transonic shocks under three dimensional perturbations of the exit pressure. The key techniques involve the decoupling the hyperbolic and elliptic components in steady relativistic Euler equations using the deformation tensor and vorticity. To address the coordinate singularities, the ``spherical projection coordinates" combining the spherical coordinates and the stereographic projection is employed. Subsequently, the Rankine-Hugoniot conditions are appropriately reformulated to determine the shock front and derive the boundary conditions on the shock front for a first-order nonlocal deformation-curl system.

</details>


### [160] [Open Inextensible Filaments in Planar Stokes Flow: Well-Posedness, Endpoint Asymptotics, and Straightening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10480)
*Han Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究平面Stokes流体中带自由端点的不可伸长开丝，建立局部与整体适定性、端点渐近展开及长时间行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平面Stokes流中带自由端点的不可伸长开丝（filament）的动力学，包括其适定性、端点附近正则性以及长时间收敛行为。

Method: 将系统化为三阶非局部曲率方程与张力椭圆方程的耦合；在满足arc-chord条件的Sobolev空间$\widetilde H^s$（$-1/2<s\le0$）中处理近临界初值；利用Wiener--Hopf分解分析端点渐近；通过能量恒等式与几何估计联系弯曲能与arc-chord常数。

Result: 证明近临界初值的局部适定性；对正时间证明Sobolev正则性提升，并导出每个自由端点处$d^{3/2}$型展开（$d$为到端点的距离）；对小初值及满足$E(0)<\pi^2/4$的有限能量初值证明整体存在与向直线的指数收敛；有限时间爆破必伴随arc-chord条件失效；整体解要么指数收敛到直线，要么arc-chord常数沿趋于无穷的时间序列趋于零。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了平面Stokes流中带自由端点开丝的完整适定性与长时间刻画，揭示端点$d^{3/2}$渐近、弯曲能-arc-chord几何估计，以及整体解收敛或arc-chord常数趋于零的二分结构。

Abstract: We study an inextensible open filament with free ends in a planar Stokes fluid. The system reduces to a third-order nonlocal curvature equation coupled to an elliptic equation for the tension. We prove local well-posedness for nearly critical initial data in supported Sobolev spaces $\widetilde H^s$, $-1/2<s\le0$, satisfying the arc-chord condition. For positive times, we prove improved Sobolev regularity and derive a $d^{3/2}$-type expansion at each free end using Wiener--Hopf factorization, where $d$ denotes the distance to that endpoint. We further prove global existence and exponential convergence to a straight filament for sufficiently small initial data and for finite-energy initial data satisfying $E(0)<π^2/4$. The finite-energy result follows from an energy identity and a geometric estimate relating the bending energy to the arc-chord constant. More generally, any finite-time breakdown must be accompanied by loss of the arc-chord condition, while every global solution either converges exponentially to a straight filament or has arc-chord constants tending to zero along a sequence of times tending to infinity.

</details>


### [161] [A density result for admissible displacements for an obstacle problem in linearised elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09570)
*Paolo Piersanti*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对用于证明线性弹性椭圆膜壳Koiter模型合理性的“密度性质”进行了改进，并指出该技术有望推广至其他障碍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有“密度性质”是论证线性弹性椭圆膜壳在受限于给定半空间条件下Koiter模型合理性的关键工具，作者希望改进该性质并扩展其适用范围。

Method: 对既有“密度性质”进行改进（数学分析/严格证明），针对线性弹性椭圆膜壳被约束于给定半空间的情形。

Result: 给出了“密度性质”的改进版本，可用于建立Koiter模型的合理性。

Conclusion: 改进后的技术具有推广潜力，可应用于其他类型的障碍问题。

Abstract: In this short note we provide an improvement of the ``density property'', which was employed to establish the justification of Koiter's model for linearly elastic elliptic membrane shells subjected to remaining confined in a prescribed half-space. This technique has the potential to be applicable to other types of obstacle problems.

</details>


### [162] [Analysis and stability of the FitzHugh-Nagumo system with rapidly oscillating Neumann boundary condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09636)
*Alexander Rodriguez,Eduardo Cerpa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带正弦边界驱动的半直线FitzHugh-Nagumo系统，用平均法在高频下将其近似为快振荡项与简化系统解的叠加，并借助定制收缩矩形法得到持久时变边界条件下的稳定性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨半直线上FitzHugh-Nagumo系统在原点处经受高频正弦Neumann边界强制时的动力学行为，并希望在持续时变的边界条件下建立稳定性结果。直接分析原系统因高振荡驱动而困难，故需寻找有效近似与稳定性工具。

Method: 采用平均法（averaging method）处理高频正弦驱动，将原系统近似为高振荡项与一个简化系统的解之和；并结合一种针对系数随空间与时间变化的抛物型方程改编的收缩矩形法（contracting rectangles method）进行稳定性分析。

Result: 当振荡驱动的频率足够高时，系统可被上述近似表示；该近似使得在持久时变边界条件下能够建立稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 平均法与定制收缩矩形法的结合为带高频时变边界驱动的FitzHugh-Nagumo系统提供了近似与稳定性分析框架，适用于系数时空变化的抛物型方程。

Abstract: In this paper we study the FitzHugh-Nagumo system posed on the half-line with a sinusoidal forcing term acting at the origin through the Neumann boun\-da\-ry condition. By applying an averaging method, we show that, if the frequency of the oscillating forcing is high enough, then
  the system can be approximated by a combination of a highly-oscillatory term and the solution of a simpler system. This approach allows us to establish stability results under persistent time-varying boundary conditions. Along with the averaging method, we use an adaptation of the contracting rectangles method, tailored to handle parabolic equations with spatially and temporally varying coefficients.

</details>


### [163] [Radial solutions of quasilinear Hardy--Emden--Fowler equations: exact decay rates, Delaunay solutions, and nonexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09724)
*Phuong Le*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文确定了拟线性Hardy–Emden–Fowler方程在外部区域上所有正解在无穷远处的渐近行为，刻画了自相似率与两个Hardy率之间的竞争，并在四个参数区域给出完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟线性、带权重的Hardy–Emden–Fowler方程正解在无穷远处的渐近行为，将Franca–Sfecci对p=2的结果推广至一般1<p<N，且不受p≤2的限制。

Method: 引入斜率变量x=-rv'/v、Y=r^{p+s}v^{q-p+1}，将方程化为上半平面{x>0}上实解析的平面系统；利用万有障碍x<ν_{+}、显式Dulac函数（在q=p*_s-1时退化为首次积分）以及显式Lyapunov函数进行分析。

Result: 给出四区域完整图景：当q>p*_s-1时每个解恰实现三种率之一，临界情况h(γ_*)=μ带对数修正并算出首项常数；当q=p*_s-1时系统可积，存在奇异自相似解、异宿"bubble"及单参数Fowler–Delaunay解族（r^{γ_*}v(r)为ln r的非平凡周期函数）；当q_S<q<p*_s-1时仅两条轨道存活；当q≤q_S时外部区域无正解。

Conclusion: 该工作为p=2情形结果的拟线性加权推广，借助斜率变量与解析平面系统方法，无需p≤2限制即可获得无穷远处所有正解的渐近分类。

Abstract: Let $N>p>1$, $s>-p$, $0<μ<\barμ:=\big(\frac{N-p}{p}\big)^{p}$ and $q>p-1$. We determine the behaviour at infinity of \emph{every} positive solution of the quasilinear Hardy--Emden--Fowler equation \[ -\big(r^{N-1}|v'|^{p-2}v'\big)'=μ\,r^{N-1-p}v^{p-1}+r^{N-1+s}v^{q}, \qquad r>R_{0}. \] Three rates compete: the self-similar rate $r^{-γ_{*}}$ with $γ_{*}=\frac{p+s}{q-p+1}$, and the two Hardy rates $r^{-ν_{\pm}}$, where $ν_{\pm}$ are the roots of $h(ν):=ν^{p-1}(N-p-(p-1)ν)=μ$. Writing $p^{*}_{s}=\frac{p(N+s)}{N-p}$ and $q_{S}=p-1+\frac{p+s}{ν_{+}}$ for the Hardy--Sobolev and Serrin exponents, we obtain a complete picture in four regimes. If $q>p^{*}_{s}-1$, every solution realises exactly one of the three rates, with a logarithmic correction in the borderline case $h(γ_{*})=μ$, and we compute the leading constants. If $q=p^{*}_{s}-1$ the system becomes integrable: besides the singular self-similar solution and a heteroclinic ``bubble'' there is a one-parameter family of Fowler--Delaunay solutions, for which $r^{γ_{*}}v(r)$ is a non-constant periodic function of $\ln r$. If $q_{S}<q<p^{*}_{s}-1$ only two orbits survive, and if $q\le q_{S}$ there is no positive solution at all in an exterior domain. This is the quasilinear, weighted counterpart of the picture obtained by Franca and Sfecci for $p=2$. We work with the slope variables $x=-rv'/v$, $Y=r^{p+s}v^{q-p+1}$, in which the equation becomes a planar system that is real-analytic on the half-plane $\{x>0\}$ for every $1<p<N$, so that no restriction of the type $p\le2$ is needed; the analysis then rests on a universal barrier $x<ν_{+}$, on an explicit Dulac function which degenerates into a first integral exactly at $q=p^{*}_{s}-1$, and on an explicit Lyapunov function.

</details>


### [164] [A Sharp Sobolev Inequality on Compact CR Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09841)
*Zhongwei Tang,Bingwei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在紧致严格伪凸CR流形上证明了带最优常数的Folland–Stein Sobolev不等式，其最优常数与Heisenberg群上的相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Heisenberg群上经典的Folland–Stein Sobolev不等式推广到一般的紧致严格伪凸CR流形，并寻求其中的最优（sharp）常数，即保持与Heisenberg群相同的最优常数。

Method: 设\((M,\theta)\)为维数\(2n+1\)的紧致严格伪凸超曲面型CR流形，齐次维数\(Q=2n+2\)。取临界Sobolev指数\(Q^*=2Q/(Q-2)\)，对\(u\in S^{1,2}(M)\)建立含\(\|\nabla_b u\|_{L^2}\)与\(\|u\|_{L^2}\)的不等式，其中梯度为次椭圆（horizontal）梯度\(\nabla_b\)。

Result: 证明存在仅依赖于\((M,\theta)\)的常数\(A>0\)，使得\(\|u\|_{L^{Q^*}(M)}^2 \le K(n)^2\|\nabla_b u\|_{L^2(M)}^2 + A\|u\|_{L^2(M)}^2\)，其中\(K(n)\)为Heisenberg群\(\mathbb H^n\)上Folland–Stein Sobolev不等式的最优常数。

Conclusion: 该不等式在一般CR流形上成立且最优常数为\(K(n)\)，说明流形的曲率等几何因素仅影响低阶项（\(L^2\)项），而不改变临界指数的sharp常数。

Abstract: For any \(n\geq 1\), let \((M,θ)\) be a compact strictly pseudoconvex hypersurface type CR manifold with dimension \(2n+1\), \(Q=2n+2\) be its homogeneous dimension. We prove the sharp Folland--Stein Sobolev inequality on \(M\): if \(Q^*=2Q/(Q-2)\), then there exists a constant \(A>0\), depending only on \((M,θ)\), such that
  \[
  \|u\|_{L^{Q^*}(M)}^2 \le K(n)^2\|\nabla_b u\|_{L^2(M)}^2 + A\|u\|_{L^2(M)}^2, \qquad u\in S^{1,2}(M).
  \]
  Here \(K(n)\) is the sharp constant in the Folland--Stein Sobolev inequality on the Heisenberg group \(\mathbb H^n\).

</details>


### [165] [Finite-time divergence of kinetic-energy fluctuations in a Schrödinger-induction flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10085)
*Weishuo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在带约束的 Schrödinger–induction 系统框架下，构造了一个由流体奇异性驱动的动力学能量涨散发散而平均动能保持有界的显式周期轨迹，并刻画了端点态的谱域正则性缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究流体集中（fluid concentration）与动能域正则性丧失之间的联系，并建立一个动力学基准，用以评估流体力学波动表述中平均能量控制的极限。

Method: 采用带约束的 Schrödinger–induction 系统；假定所引用的 Navier–Stokes 构造具有零初速及其精确外部剖面；由常数归一化波函数与零联络构造周期轨迹；体作用力在归一化奇异时刻 t=1 处存在光滑时空延拓；通过在振荡修正全部消失的精确外部区域上对完整速度模的四次方积分来推导增长下界；并固定常数波函数规范，构造弱 L^2 联络极限及其正自伴动力学算子。

Result: 概率密度保持常数、平均物质动能保持有界，而规范不变的动能方差发散，其下界增长阶为 (1-t)^{-1/2-5h}（h 由源构造固定）；在固定常数波函数规范后，存在唯一的弱 L^2 联络极限（但不在 L^4 中），并可经由相关磁形式构造其正自伴动力学算子；归一化端点态属于形式域但不属于算子域，其第一动力学谱矩有限而第二谱矩无穷。

Conclusion: 结果表明流体集中会导致动力学算子域正则性的丧失，并为评估流体力学波动表述中平均能量控制的局限提供了动力学基准，凸显了平均动能有界性不足以保证谱域层次的正则性。

Abstract: In this study, we use a constrained Schrödinger--induction system to investigate kinetic-energy fluctuations induced by a fluid singularity. Assuming the cited Navier--Stokes construction with zero initial velocity and its exact exterior profile, we construct a periodic trajectory from a constant normalized wave function and zero connection. The prescribed body force admits a smooth space--time extension across the normalized singular time $t=1$. In this construction, the probability density remains constant and the mean matter kinetic energy stays bounded, whereas the gauge-invariant kinetic-energy variance diverges. We prove a lower growth bound of order $(1-t)^{-1/2-5h}$, with $h$ fixed by the source construction, by integrating the fourth power of the complete velocity's magnitude over an exact exterior region where all oscillatory corrections vanish. After fixing a gauge with constant wave function, we identify a unique weak $L^2$ connection limit outside $L^4$ and construct its positive self-adjoint kinetic operator through the associated magnetic form. We further show that the normalized endpoint state belongs to the form domain but not the operator domain: its first kinetic spectral moment is finite, whereas its second is infinite. These results connect fluid concentration to a loss of kinetic-domain regularity and provide a dynamical benchmark for assessing the limits of mean-energy control in hydrodynamic wave formulations.

</details>


### [166] [Logarithmic Stability for an Inverse Source Problem in a Coupled Nonlinear Helmholtz System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09845)
*Hongcan Ye,Lei Zhang,Ting Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二次谐波生成驱动的双模非线性Helmholtz系统的逆源问题，证明在单个固定小边界态处的Fréchet导数数据下，源项的唯一性及条件单对数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次谐波生成等非线性光学成像中，测量数据往往仅为边界态的一次变分，需要确定能否由此唯一重建内部源并给出稳定性，这是非线性逆源问题的核心难点。

Method: 将第一边界变分转化为对称2×2矩阵Schrödinger算子的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，把已知Helmholtz能量吸收进矩阵势，使用共享零相位的零能量复几何光学解，结合双线性Alessandrini恒等式得到背景场项的Fourier估计；再通过低于3/2指数的零延拓、低/高频分解及H^{-2}与先验H^s界之间的插值获得稳定性模。

Result: 对实值小数据、已知且远离零的极化率以及支撑于固定紧集的源，证明了唯一性，并给出了条件单对数稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 单个固定小边界态处非线性Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射的Fréchet导数足以唯一确定紧支源，并达到单对数型稳定性，为非线性光学逆源问题提供了新的理论保证。

Abstract: We study an inverse source problem for a two-mode nonlinear Helmholtz system motivated by second-harmonic generation. The datum is the full first Fréchet derivative of the nonlinear Dirichlet-to-Neumann map at one fixed, common, small boundary state, which need not be zero. For real-valued small data, a known susceptibility bounded away from zero, and sources supported in a fixed compact subset of the domain, we prove uniqueness and a conditional single-logarithmic stability estimate. The first boundary variation is the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map of a symmetric $2\times2$ matrix Schrödinger operator. Absorbing the two known Helmholtz energies into its matrix potential permits the use of standard zero-energy complex geometrical optics solutions with a common null phase geometry. A bilinear Alessandrini identity then gives a uniform Fourier estimate for the two entries containing the background fields. Zero extension below the exponent $3/2$, a low/high frequency splitting, and interpolation between $H^{-2}$ and the a priori $H^s$ source bound yield the stated stability modulus.

</details>


### [167] [Positive Solutions for an Indefinite-Weight Minkowski Mean Curvature Neumann Problem: Multiplicity and Asymptotic Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09894)
*Ricardo Ziegele*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究Neumann边值问题$-\operatorname{div}(\nabla u/\sqrt{1-|\nabla u|^2})=\lambda a(x)g(u)$，用Szulkin非光滑泛函理论证明正解存在性并给出渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究含1-拉普拉斯型算子（预设平均曲率型）的Neumann问题，权重$a$可变号且$\int_\Omega a<0$，需要处理非变分/非光滑结构与约束饱和现象。

Method: 基于Szulkin非光滑泛函理论研究全局极小与山路临界点：全局极小$u_\lambda^{(l)}$（负能量）与山路临界点$u_\lambda^{(s)}$（正能量）；随后在$g(u)=|u|^{p-2}u$情形分析$\lambda\to+\infty$的渐近，导出能量率与极限剖面方程。

Result: 在适当假设下，对充分大$\lambda>0$存在两个正解；模型情形$g(u)=|u|^{p-2}u$中，山路能级$c_\lambda=O(\lambda^{-2/(p-2)})$，$u_\lambda^{(s)}\to0$于$C(\overline\Omega)$；且$u_\lambda^{(l)}\to u_\infty$一致，其中$u_\infty$解一个约束极大化问题，满足$\|\nabla u_\infty\|_{L^\infty(\Omega)}=1$，并在梯度约束非激活且$a\neq0$的连通开集上为常数。

Conclusion: 该问题在充分大$\lambda$下具有双正解分支；小解消失而大解收敛到饱和梯度约束的极限剖面，极限在“非激活区”呈分段常数结构。

Abstract: We study the Neumann boundary value problem $$ -\operatorname{div}\left(\frac{\nabla u}{\sqrt{1-|\nabla u|^2}}\right) = λa(x)g(u) \quad \text{in } Ω, \qquad \frac{\nabla u}{\sqrt{1-|\nabla u|^2}}\cdot \mathbf n = 0 \quad \text{on } \partialΩ, $$ where $Ω\subset \mathbb R^N$ is a bounded convex domain, $a\in L^\infty(Ω)$ is an indefinite weight with $\int_Ωa < 0$, and $g$ is a nonlinearity. Under suitable assumptions on $g$, we prove the existence of two positive solutions for sufficiently large $λ>0$, using Szulkin's theory for nonsmooth functionals: a global minimizer $u_λ^{(l)}$ with negative energy, and a mountain-pass critical point $u_λ^{(s)}$ with positive energy. Furthermore, we study the asymptotic behaviour of both solutions as $λ\to +\infty$ in the model case $g(u) = |u|^{p-2} u$. We show that the mountain-pass energy level decays at the explicit rate $c_λ= O(λ^{-2/(p-2)})$, and that $u_λ^{(s)} \to 0$ in $C(\overlineΩ)$; moreover, we prove that $u_λ^{(l)} \to u_\infty$ uniformly, where $u_\infty$ solves a constrained maximization problem. The limiting profile $u_\infty$ saturates the geometric constraint, $\|\nabla u_\infty\|_{L^\infty(Ω)}=1$, and on every connected open set where the gradient constraint is inactive and $a\neq 0$, the function $u_\infty$ is constant.

</details>


### [168] [On the rotation-two-component $b$-family shallow-water system: derivation and wave-breaking analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09900)
*Xingxing Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将旋转二分量b族浅水波系统从含科里奥利力与常涡量的Green-Naghdi型方程导出，并研究其特例旋转二分量Degasperis-Procesi系统的爆破准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转二分量Degasperis-Procesi系统同时存在两个难点：它不具有H^1×L^2型守恒量，且含有科里奥利力诱导的高阶非线性耦合项，因此难以沿用已有的守恒律方法研究其爆破行为。

Method: 从含科里奥利力与常涡量的Green-Naghdi型方程出发推导旋转二分量b族系统；针对旋转二分量DP系统，引入辅助变量并在不使用任何守恒律的情况下建立u的局部L^∞估计，据此得到两个不同的爆破准则。

Result: 建立了旋转二分量DP系统的局部L^∞估计与两个爆破准则；当科里奥利力消失时，结果退化为2DP系统的已知准则，并为2DP系统与DP方程提供新的准则；结果可自然推广到周期情形及旋转b族系统的相关特例。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法克服了无守恒律与高阶非线性耦合的双重困难，确立了旋转b族系统作为b族旋转推广的地位，并给出了若干新的爆破准则。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive the rotation-two-component $b$-family shallow-water wave system from the Green--Naghdi type equations incorporating the effects of the Coriolis force and constant vorticity. The system admits, among its special cases, the two-component Degasperis--Procesi (2DP) system as a reduction, thereby confirming its genuine role as a rotational extension of the $b$-family. We then focus on a specific reduction, the rotation-two-component Degasperis-Procesi system, whose particular interest lies in the fact that it combines two independent sources of difficulty: unlike the two-component Camassa-Holm type system, it possesses no conserved $H^1\times L^2$-type quantity, and in contrast to the standard 2DP system, it contains a higher-order nonlinear coupling term induced by the Coriolis force. To overcome this double difficulty, we introduce an auxiliary variable and establish a local $L^\infty$-estimate for $u$ without using any conservation law, based on which we derive two distinct blow-up criteria. When the Coriolis force vanishes, our results recover known criteria for the 2DP system and also provide new criteria for both the 2DP system and Degasperis-Procesi equation. The results extend naturally to the periodic setting and to relevant special cases of the rotational $b$-family system.

</details>


### [169] [Superlinear nonlocal diffusion systems for multispecies populations: well-posedness and discrete chain rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09921)
*Peter Hirvonen,Ansgar Jüngel,Annamaria Pollino*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文分析了一个描述多种群动力学的非局部扩散系统，该系统可逼近SKT模型，在有界区域内证明了强解的整体存在性与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画多种群种群动力学并逼近经典的Shigesada-Kawasaki-Teramoto（SKT）模型，需要研究相应的非局部扩散系统，以理解其解的适定性及非局部到局部极限行为。

Method: 研究有界区域上由Andreu-Mazón-Rossi-Toledo型积分微分方程组构成的模型，其中非局部交叉扩散算子作用于非线性扩散势。通过熵不等式导出的先验估计证明存在性，并发展新的离散链式法则不等式；唯一性采用对偶论证；最后进行一维数值模拟。

Result: 建立了强解的整体存在性与唯一性；新的离散链式法则不等式为对应局部SKT系统的结构保持有限体积格式提供了基础；一维数值模拟展示了非局部到局部的极限以及超线性与次线性扩散势下解的定性行为。

Conclusion: 该非局部扩散系统的强解适定性得到证明，相关离散链式法则不等式还可用于构造结构保持的有限体积格式，数值结果验证了理论并揭示了扩散势类型对解行为的影响。

Abstract: A nonlocal diffusion system describing the dynamics of multi-species populations and approximating the Shigesada-Kawasaki-Teramoto (SKT) model is analyzed in a bounded domain. The model consists of a system of integro-differential equations of Andreu-Mazón-Rossi-Toledo type, in which a nonlocal cross-diffusion operator acts on nonlinear diffusion potentials. We establish the global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions. The existence proof relies on suitable a priori estimates derived from an entropy inequality, whose proof requires the development of novel discrete chain-rule inequalities. As a by-product, these inequalities provide the basis for the construction of structure-preserving finite-volume schemes for the corresponding local SKT system. Uniqueness of solutions is established by means of a duality argument. Finally, one-dimensional numerical simulations illustrate the nonlocal-to-local limit and the qualitative behavior of solutions for superlinear and sublinear diffusion potentials.

</details>


### [170] [The $C^{p'}$-regularity conjecture near $p=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09966)
*Se-Chan Lee,Taehun Lee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 $p>2$ 且充分接近 $2$ 时，于任意维数证明了 $C^{p'}$-正则性猜想，并给出了最优梯度 Hölder 指数的一阶渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $p$-调和函数的梯度正则性（$C^{p'}$-正则性猜想）是正则性理论中的核心问题，其在高维、$p>2$ 情形下的最优 Hölder 指数刻画长期未完全解决。

Method: 证明结合了紧性论证、极限剖面的调和刚性（harmonic rigidity）分析，以及关于梯度过剩（gradient excess）一阶变分的尖锐一致间隙估计。

Result: 建立了 $p$-调和函数梯度的改进 Hölder 估计；在任意维数下确定了最优梯度 Hölder 指数的一阶渐近；并在 $p$ 充分接近 $2$ 时证明了 $C^{p'}$-正则性猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $C^{p'}$-正则性猜想在 $p$ 接近 $2$ 的所有维数下解决，并揭示了最优 Hölder 指数随 $p\to 2$ 的一阶渐近结构。

Abstract: We prove the $C^{p'}$-regularity conjecture in every dimension when $p>2$ is sufficiently close to $2$. To this end, we establish improved Hölder estimates for the gradients of $p$-harmonic functions. These estimates also determine the first-order asymptotics of the optimal gradient Hölder exponent in every dimension. The proof combines compactness, harmonic rigidity of the limiting profiles, and a sharp uniform gap estimate for the first variation of the gradient excess.

</details>


### [171] [Classification of multi-solitons in one sense of time for the 5D energy-critical wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09967)
*Yvan Martel,Frank Merle*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对5维聚焦能量临界波动方程在正向时间内呈多孤子结构的解进行分类，证明其必为具有共线且两两不同速度的行波叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚焦能量临界波动方程的多孤子分类是色散方程渐近行为研究的核心问题，但高维（尤其5维）情形因临界指数与慢衰减特性而尚不清楚，需要建立系统分类。

Method: 考虑方程 ∂_t^2 u − Δu − |u|^{4/3}u = 0 于 [T_0,∞)×R^5；假设解在 L^2 意义下以 t^{-1/2+} 速率逼近给定有限族行波 {W_k} 的叠加，其中 W_k 基于显式基态 W(x)=(1+|x|^2/15)^{-3/2}，且速度共线、两两不同。通过渐近分析与能量/单调量估计对该误差控制下的解进行刻画与分类。

Result: 在上述误差假设与速度条件下，证明此类多孤子解必为相应行波叠加，即多孤子结构唯一由给定行波族确定，并获得误差 t^{-1/2+} 的定量刻画。

Conclusion: 5维聚焦能量临界波动方程的正向多孤子解被完全分类为共线、两两不同速度行波的叠加，为高维临界波动方程多孤子理论提供了分类结果与定量框架。

Abstract: We classify the multi-solitons, in the positive sense of time, of the focusing energy-critical wave equation in space dimension $5$, more precisely the solutions $u(t,x)$ of the equation \[ \partial_t^2 u - Δu - |u|^{\frac 4{3}} u = 0, \quad (t,x)\in [T_0,\infty)\times {\mathbb R}^5, \] which satisfy the estimate \[ \left\|\nabla_{t,x} \left(u(t) - \sum_{k} W_k(t) \right) \right\|_{L^2} \lesssim t^{-\frac12^+} \quad \hbox{for all $t\gg 1$}. \] Here, $\{W_k\}_k$ is any given finite family of traveling waves, based on the explicit ground state solution \[ W(x) = \left(1+ \frac{|x|^2}{15}\right)^{-\frac32} \] with collinear two-by-two different velocities.

</details>


### [172] [Global well-posedness for the derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10005)
*Hajer Bahouri,Galina Perelman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在圆周上研究导数非线性薛定谔方程，证明其在H^1(T)中全局适定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导数非线性薛定谔方程（DNLS）是数学物理中的重要模型，其在低正则空间中的适定性（尤其是全局适定性）是核心难题，此前H^1情形尚未解决。

Method: 在圆周（一维环面T）上考虑DNLS方程，通过引入新的可积结构/规范变换与低正则分析技术，证明H^1(T)中的全局适定性。

Result: 证明了DNLS方程在H^1(T)中是全局适定的。

Conclusion: 该结果将DNLS在环面上的全局适定性推进到H^1正则性水平。

Abstract: We consider the derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation on the circle, and prove that it is globally well-posed in the Sobolev space $H^1(\TT)$.

</details>


### [173] [Global nonlinear stability of the plasma-vacuum free boundary problem for the 2D ideal incompressible MHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10009)
*Yuan Cai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维等离子体-真空界面问题，在强水平磁场条件下证明了理想不可压缩MHD方程自由边界的全局非线性稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体-真空界面问题是理想Euler型方程的自由边界问题，其全局非线性稳定性具有重要理论与应用价值。作者旨在强水平磁场下建立二维情形的全局时间稳定性。

Method: 等离子体区由理想不可压缩磁流体动力学方程描述，真空区由pre-Maxwell动力学描述（未知量简化为零）。在等离子体区施加强水平磁场，通过分析域内流体动力学与自由界面动力学之间的相互作用以及问题内在结构来完成证明。

Result: 在强水平磁场假设下，证明了二维等离子体-真空界面的全局时间非线性稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了强水平磁场下二维等离子体-真空界面的全局非线性稳定性，深化了对理想Euler型自由边界问题中流体与界面耦合机制的理解。

Abstract: We address the plasma-vacuum interface problem in the two dimensional space. The plasma region is governed by the ideal incompressible magnetohydrodynamic equations. The vacuum region is described by the pre-Maxwell dynamics, with the unknowns set to zero for simplicity. This is a free boundary problem of ideal Euler type equations. Under the strong horizontal magnetic field in the plasma region, we prove the global in time nonlinear stability of the two dimensional plasma-vacuum interface. The proof relies on understanding the interplay between the dynamics of the fluids inside the domain and those on the free interface, as well as the inherent structures of the problem.

</details>


### [174] [Secular Instability and the Turning-Point Principle for Rigidly Rotating Viscous Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10098)
*Ming Cheng,Zhiwu Lin,Yucong Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究刚性旋转黏性星的轴对称稳定性，证明线性化NSP生成元的失稳指数（计Riesz代数重数）等于固定质量与总角动量下增广能量的Morse指数，并据此揭示黏性耗散可使旋转星先于无黏星失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转星的稳定性判据长期依赖无黏（Euler–Poisson）模型，但真实恒星存在黏性耗散。黏性既耗散能量又重分布角动量，其对稳定性的影响方向不明：耗散通常稳定化，但角动量重分布可能去掉无黏约束而致失稳。作者希望建立严格的黏性星轴对称稳定性理论，澄清耗散是否以及何时会“反常” destabilize 旋转星。

Method: 1. 以自由边界Navier–Stokes–Poisson (NSP)系统刻画刚性旋转黏性星。2. 证明抽象Kelvin–Tait–Chetaev型定理：对带阻尼的陀螺方程，当负刚度指数有限时，线性生成元失稳指数（含Riesz代数重数）等于增广能量的Morse指数；定理不要求紧性、不要求零点谱隙，刚度核可为无穷维。3. 应用于NSP：黏性耗散退化，其轴对称核由刚性轴向平移与刚性旋转生成，先投影掉该核再套用抽象定理。4. 利用守恒律与Routh约化，将所得失稳指数回推到完整NSP生成元。

Result: 对固定总角动量的慢旋转分支，黏性失稳始于第一个非退化球对称质量极大值的延拓点；而对任意充分小的非零角动量，相应的Euler–Poisson星在该极大值之后的一段区间内仍保持轴对称谱稳定。因此黏性星会在对应无黏星失稳之前先失稳，两稳定性阈值出现分离。

Conclusion: 黏性耗散可通过角动量重分布去除无黏模型对物质角动量分布的限制（同时保持总角动量守恒），从而在无黏星仍稳定时使旋转星失稳。该结果给出黏性与无黏稳定性阈值分离的机制，并提供了分析退化耗散陀螺系统稳定性的通用抽象工具。

Abstract: We study axisymmetric stability of rigidly rotating viscous stars modeled by the free-boundary Navier--Stokes--Poisson (NSP) system. The unstable index of the linearized NSP generator, counted with Riesz algebraic multiplicity, equals the Morse index of the augmented energy at fixed mass and total angular momentum. We prove an abstract Kelvin--Tait--Chetaev theorem for damped gyroscopic equations with finite negative stiffness index, requiring no compactness assumptions and no spectral gap at zero; the stiffness kernel may be infinite-dimensional. In the NSP application, the viscous dissipation is degenerate: its axisymmetric kernel, generated by rigid axial translation and rigid rotation, is projected out before applying the abstract theorem, while conservation laws and a Routh reduction transfer the resulting instability index back to the full NSP generator. For slowly rotating branches with fixed total angular momentum, viscous instability begins at the continuation of a first nondegenerate spherical mass maximum. By contrast, for every sufficiently small nonzero angular momentum, the corresponding Euler--Poisson star remains axisymmetrically spectrally stable on an interval beyond this maximum. Thus dissipation can destabilize a rotating star before the corresponding inviscid star becomes unstable. This separation of the stability thresholds results from viscous redistribution of angular momentum, which removes the inviscid constraints on its material distribution while preserving its total value.

</details>


### [175] [Controlled harmonic map heat flow and blow-up from the disk to the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10129)
*Shengquan Xiang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究圆盘到球面的一同伦类调和映射热流的Dirichlet边界控制问题，引入"控制适配粘合策略"，构造显式上剖面与良制备边界迹，刻画边界控制对爆破与整体存在性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射热流在无控制情形下可能发生有限时间爆破，本文旨在探究边界控制能否以及在何种条件下阻止或引发爆破，从而建立爆破判据与整体存在性结果。

Method: 采用控制适配的粘合（gluing）策略：构造显式的上剖面与良制备（well-prepared）边界迹。对每一条此类上剖面，若非负初值低于该剖面且边界值大于π，则在相应常值迹下解必爆破；若控制低于对应的良制备边界迹，则解整体存在。

Result: 证明了边界控制可决定爆破与否，时间导数一致有界的控制还能阻止无穷时刻的集中现象；边界控制可将任意非负初值在任意给定时刻之前驱动至爆破，而有界控制无法阻止适当快的集中。

Conclusion: 边界控制在调和映射热流的爆破现象中起到决定性作用，存在精确的阈值型判据区分爆破与整体存在，为热流的可控性提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: We study the one-corotational harmonic map heat flow from the disk to the sphere with a Dirichlet boundary control. We introduce a control-adapted gluing strategy to prevent blow-up. More precisely, we construct explicit upper profiles and well-prepared boundary traces such that every nonnegative datum below one of these profiles and having boundary value above $π$ blows up under the corresponding constant trace, whereas the corresponding solution is global under every control below the associated well-prepared boundary trace. Controls with uniformly bounded time derivative also prevent infinite-time concentration. We also show that boundary control can drive any nonnegative datum to blow-up before any prescribed time, whereas bounded controls cannot prevent suitably fast concentration.

</details>


### [176] [Bramson correction and convergence to the critical wave for Fisher-KPP equations on $\mathbb Z^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10143)
*Yuanyang Hu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究高维整数格上具紧支集初值的Fisher-KPP方程，证明前沿沿各坐标轴出现对数延迟，过渡区宽度一致有界，并在固定宽度半管中收敛到最小速度行波的平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维离散格点Fisher-KPP方程中，紧支集初值引发的传播前沿位置与连续情形存在差异，特别是沿坐标轴方向的延迟量及过渡区宽度尚需刻画。

Method: 采用加权估计、倾斜随机游走界以及乘积下解构造；不假设反应项凹性。

Result: 证明前沿沿每个带符号坐标轴呈对数延迟，过渡区宽度一致有界；在每个围绕轴的固定宽度半管上，解收敛到最小速度格点行波的平移且相位有界；并获得各方向带对数修正的上界。

Conclusion: 高维格点Fisher-KPP传播沿轴向具有对数延迟与有界宽度过渡区，且在轴附近半管中具有行波渐近行为，反应项凹性并非必要。

Abstract: We consider Fisher-KPP equations with nearest-neighbor diffusion on $\mathbb Z^d$, $d\geq2$, with nonzero finitely supported initial data. We prove a logarithmic delay of the front along each signed coordinate axis and show that the transition region has uniformly bounded width. On every fixed-width half-tube around an axis, the solution converges to translates of the minimal-speed lattice traveling wave, with a bounded phase. We also obtain an upper bound with a logarithmic correction in every direction. The proof uses weighted estimates, bounds for tilted random walks, and a product lower solution. Concavity of the reaction is not assumed.

</details>


### [177] [The shallow--water limit of linear water waves over a discontinuous bottom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10170)
*Martin Oen Paulsen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了带陡底台阶上线性水波的零色散极限，将浅水方程刻画为透射问题，并描述台阶间断附近产生的局部色散效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在底形存在不连续（尖锐台阶）时，线性水波方程的零色散极限是否成立，以及由不连续性引发的局部色散效应，从而弥补底部奇异性对浅水极限和色散修正影响的空白。

Method: 构建基于Dirichlet--Neumann算子分数次幂的抽象泛函框架，用于二维Lipschitz曲底流体域；利用改造的Rellich恒等式刻画低正则能量空间。对台阶几何采用显式共形映射，获得Grisvard位移定理的版本，并推导势函数奇异部分的显式公式。

Result: 在浅水参数下得到一致估计；识别出由台阶产生的狭窄过渡尺度，浅水透射问题的收敛在远离过渡区成立，而台阶附近保留局部色散修正；数值上验证该差异并识别出台阶诱导的色散边界层。

Conclusion: 在强色散 regime 下，作者进一步严格推导出一个显式描述小台阶边界层的色散模型，说明零色散极限与局部色散效应可被统一刻画。

Abstract: We rigorously justify the zero--dispersion limit of linear water waves over a sharp bottom step, recovering the shallow-water equations as a transmission problem, and describe the localized dispersive effects generated near the discontinuity. To obtain estimates uniform in the shallow-water parameter, we study an abstract functional framework for the linear water waves equations in two-dimensional fluid domains with a Lipschitz curved bottom. The framework is based on fractional powers of the Dirichlet--Neumann operator, and we characterize low-regularity energy spaces using adapted Rellich identities. At higher regularity, the associated velocity potential develops corner singularities.
  For the step geometry, we use an explicit conformal mapping to obtain a version of Grisvard's shift theorem, where we derive an explicit formula for the singular part of the potential. The dependence on the horizontal variable reveals a narrow transition scale generated by the step. The convergence toward the shallow-water transmission problem holds away from this transition region, while a localized dispersive correction remains near the step. We illustrate this discrepancy numerically and identify the resulting step-induced dispersive boundary layer. Building on these observations, we consider the strongly dispersive regime and rigorously derive a dispersive model that explicitly describes this boundary layer for a small step.

</details>


### [178] [A geometric approach to nonlocal 2-Hessian equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10195)
*Fernando Charro,María del Mar González,María Soria-Carro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非局部2-Hessian方程（由分数阶拉普拉斯算子线性形变的下确界定义），刻画系数矩阵类与退化几何结构，证明正右端项下仍保持一致椭圆性并给出正则性估计，且结果稳定趋于局部情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前文献对该非局部2-Hessian方程的研究假设较强（如要求凸性等），且适用范围受限。作者希望在更弱假设下、覆盖全范围 s∈(0,1) 且无需解凸性，建立一致椭圆性与正则性理论，并统一 s→1 的局部极限情形。

Method: 对由分数阶拉普拉斯算子线性形变下确界定义的非局部2-Hessian方程，刻画决定算子行为的系数矩阵类 A_2，并给出其可能退化性的几何描述；发展新的几何方法，将局部半凹性与2-凸性概念推广为非局部对应物。

Result: 主要定理表明，在严格正右端项下，非局部2-Hessian方程仍一致椭圆，从而得到正则性估计；结果在比以往文献更弱的假设下成立，适用于全范围 s∈(0,1)，且不要求解的凸性；所有结果在 s→1 时稳定并恢复局部情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非局部2-Hessian方程在弱假设下的一致椭圆性与正则性理论，其几何方法即使在局部情形下也是新的，可能适用于更广泛的非局部完全非线性方程与曲率型问题。

Abstract: We study a nonlocal 2-Hessian equation, given by an infimum of linear deformations of the fractional Laplacian, that is, $\inf_{A\in {A}_2}Δ^s (u\circ A)(A^{-1}x)$. We characterize the class of coefficient matrices ${A}_2$, which determines the behavior of the operator, and provide a detailed geometric description of the possible degeneracies. Our main theorem shows that the nonlocal 2-Hessian equation remains uniformly elliptic for strictly positive right-hand sides, which leads to regularity estimates. The results hold under weaker hypotheses than those previously considered in the literature, and for the full range $s\in(0,1)$. In particular, no convexity on the solutions is required. Our hypotheses can be interpreted as nonlocal counterparts of the local notions of semiconcavity and 2-convexity. Moreover, all the results are stable as $s\to1$, recovering the local case. The geometric methods developed here are new, even in the local setting, and may be relevant to a broader class of nonlocal fully nonlinear equations and curvature-type problems.

</details>


### [179] [Identifying unknown time delay and spatially varying coefficients in a reaction-diffusion equation from boundary measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10222)
*Ming-Hui Ding,Hongyu Liu,Catharine W. K. Lo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究含多重时滞项（滞后扩散、滞后时间导数、滞后源项）的线性反应扩散系统的反问题，仅利用边界通量测量同时恢复未知时滞 τ>0 与空间异质系数，并首次在时滞抛物型PDE框架下实现二者联合反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于时滞方程反问题的工作通常假设 τ 已知，且局限于常微分方程或抽象框架，无法处理具有空间变系数的抛物型偏微分方程；本文旨在突破这一限制，在更贴近实际的时空耦合框架下联合反演时滞与系数。

Method: 利用初始历史与边界数据不相容所产生的奇异时间行为来识别时滞 τ；在 τ 确定后，针对时间无关系数 p=p(x)、q=q(x)，借助 Carleman 不等式建立 Lipschitz 稳定性估计。

Result: 成功从边界通量数据中同时恢复未知时滞 τ 与空间依赖系数，并给出了时间无关系数情形下的 Lipschitz 稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 这是首个在时滞抛物型偏微分方程中，仅由边界数据同时恢复未知时滞与空间依赖系数的结果，拓展了时滞方程反问题的适用范围。

Abstract: This work investigates an inverse problem for a general class of linear reaction-diffusion systems incorporating multiple delayed contributions, namely retarded diffusion, retarded time derivatives, and retarded source terms. The objective is to simultaneously recover the unknown time lag $τ>0$ and spatially heterogeneous coefficients from boundary flux measurements alone. The recovery strategy exploits the singular temporal behavior generated by an incompatibility between the prescribed initial history and the boundary data. In contrast to prior inverse problems for delay equations, which assume $τ$ known and are confined to ODE or abstract settings, our approach operates in a parabolic PDE framework with spatially varying coefficients. Once $τ$ is identified, we establish a Lipschitz stability estimate via a Carleman inequality, in the case of time-independent coefficients $p=p(x)$, $q=q(x)$. This is the first result to simultaneously recover an unknown delay and spatially dependent coefficients in a delayed parabolic PDE from boundary data.

</details>


### [180] [Convergence Rate toward Rarefaction Wave under Periodic Perturbation for Generalized Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10223)
*Lin Chang,Yuling Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究广义Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers方程在空间周期扰动下的稀疏波稳定性，证明小扰动下解趋于稀疏波，核心是构造携带相同振荡的拟解并利用能量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期扰动在远场持续振荡，使得解与稀疏波之差无法直接落入可积或衰减空间，因此此类扰动下的稳定性是一个有趣且重要但具挑战性的问题。

Method: 构造与解具有相同振荡结构的合适拟解（ansatz），利用解与拟解之间的抵消使扰动落入某个Sobolev空间，再通过能量方法获得非线性稳定性。

Result: 在初始扰动关于稀疏波适当小的条件下，系统的解趋于稀疏波。

Conclusion: 广义Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers方程在空间周期扰动下稀疏波具有非线性稳定性，构造携带相同振荡的拟解是处理远场振荡扰动的关键。

Abstract: In this paper, a rarefaction wave under space-periodic perturbation for the generalized Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers equation is considered. It is shown that if the initial perturbation around the rarefaction wave is suitably small, then the solution of the system tends to the rarefaction wave. The stability of solution under periodic perturbation is an interesting and important problem since the perturbation keeps oscillating at the far fields.
  The key of proof is to construct a suitable ansatz carrying the same oscillation as the solution. Then we can find cancellations between solutions and ansatz such that the perturbation belongs to some Sobolev space. The nonlinear stability can be obtained by the energy method.

</details>


### [181] [Quantitative analysis of ground states for the fractional logarithmic Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10254)
*Xiaoming An,Shuangjie Peng,Fulin Zhong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究分数阶对数薛定谔方程 (-Δ)^s Q = Q log Q 在 R^N 上的正基态，证明了基态在平移意义下唯一且非退化，并应用于分数阶对数Sobolev不等式及其等号情形刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶对数薛定谔方程基态的唯一性与非退化性是理解其变分结构与稳定性分析的核心问题。主要障碍在于势函数 -1 - log Q 在 R^N 上无界，无法直接套用现有的分数阶薛定谔算子径向振荡理论（通常要求势有界）。

Method: 采用有界势逼近处理无界势 -1 - log Q 带来的困难；利用相应二次型具有Morse指数一以及角向分解，证明非退化性 ker L_Q = span{∂_{x_1}Q, ..., ∂_{x_N}Q}；基于对数基态的孤立性与分数幂方程的唯一性理论，通过次临界幂非线性变分逼近证明唯一性。

Result: 对任意 N ≥ 1、0 < s < 1，证明正基态在平移和伸缩意义下唯一且非退化，即线性化算子 L_Q = (-Δ)^s - 1 - log Q 的核仅由平移方向张成。作为应用，建立了带最佳常数的分数阶对数Sobolev不等式并完整刻画了所有取等情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了分数阶对数薛定谔方程基态的唯一性与非退化性问题，克服了无界势带来的技术障碍，并将结果成功应用于分数阶对数Sobolev不等式的最优常数及等号情形刻画。

Abstract: Let $N\geq1$ and $0<s<1$. We study positive ground states of the fractional logarithmic Schrödinger equation \begin{equation*} (-Δ)^sQ=Q\log Q \quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^N. \end{equation*} We prove that for every $N\geq1$ and $0<s<1$, the positive ground state is unique up to translations and nondegenerate. More precisely, for the linearized operator $L_Q=(-Δ)^s-1-\log Q$, it holds that \begin{equation*} \ker L_Q=\operatorname{span}\{\partial_{x_1}Q,\cdots,\partial_{x_N}Q\}. \end{equation*} A main difficulty is that the potential $-1-\log Q$ is unbounded in $\mathbb{R}^N$, which prevents a direct application of the available radial oscillation theory for fractional Schrödinger operators with bounded potentials. We overcome this difficulty by a bounded-potential approximation. Using also the fact that the associated quadratic form has Morse index one and an angular decomposition, we obtain the nondegeneracy. Based on the isolation of logarithmic ground states and the uniqueness theory for the fractional power equation, we prove uniqueness by a variational approximation with subcritical power nonlinearities. As an application, we establish sharp fractional logarithmic Sobolev inequalities and characterize all cases of equality.

</details>


### [182] [Distribution of Singular Data Generated by Compact Forced Navier-Stokes Blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10262)
*Shaozhen Cao,Zhuoni Chi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究OpenAI构造的紧致光滑外力驱动Navier--Stokes爆破解所生成奇异数据的分布，证明在三维环面上，给定粘性和时间horizon时，从静止产生经典爆破的光滑外力在继承的时间积分空间H^s拓扑中稠密当且仅当s<1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有OpenAI构造的紧致光滑外力Navier--Stokes爆破解，但尚不清楚它所生成的奇异数据的分布规律，以及在不同Sobolev正则性拓扑下光滑外力能否逼近奇异数据。

Method: 通过向任意正则参考轨迹中精确插入一个局域向量势，移除集中奇异包周围的背景，使所有非线性交叉项消失，从而使修改后的外力在奇异时刻保持光滑；结合全支撑构造、导数估计、分数阶Sobolev标度和经典寿命论证；另用临界外力bootstrap、H^1估计和高Sobolev延拓给出非稠密性所需的开集。

Result: 光滑外力在继承的时间积分空间H^s拓扑中稠密当且仅当s<1/2；插入轨迹在能量和耗散范数下强收敛；在s=1/2及以上给出非稠密性的正则开集；并获得扩展数据投影、固定光滑初速切片、混合外力范数、无穷维变分和内壁无滑移实现等结果。

Conclusion: 该构造通过改变外力而非对单一给定外力分类奇异初速度，刻画了三维环面上光滑外力从静止产生经典爆破数据的稠密性阈值s=1/2。

Abstract: Starting from the compact, smoothly forced Navier--Stokes blowup solution constructed by OpenAI, we study the distribution of the singular data it generates. For fixed viscosity and time horizon on the three-dimensional torus, smooth forces producing classical breakdown from rest by that time are dense in the inherited time-integrated spatial $H^s$ topology if and only if $s<1/2$ (the norms are defined in Section~\ref{sec:intro}). The positive result follows from an exact insertion into every regular reference trajectory. A localized vector potential removes the background around a concentrated singular packet, so all nonlinear cross terms vanish and the modified force remains smooth through the singular time. We give the full support construction, derivative estimates, fractional Sobolev scaling and classical-lifespan argument. The inserted trajectories converge strongly in the energy and dissipation norm. A separate critical-force bootstrap, followed by an $H^1$ estimate and high-Sobolev continuation, supplies the regular open set needed for non-density at and above $s=1/2$. Further results describe extended-data projections, every fixed smooth initial-velocity slice, mixed force norms, infinite-dimensional variations and interior no-slip realizations. The construction varies the force; it does not classify singular initial velocities for a single prescribed force.

</details>


### [183] [Singular Forces on the Whole Space: Sobolev Density Thresholds and Energy Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10269)
*Shaozhen Cao,Zhuoni Chi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 基于既有的紧致、光滑受迫 Navier–Stokes 爆破构造，本文在全空间 $\mathbb R^3$ 上给出分布型结果：对固定正黏度与给定期限，导致经典解从静止爆破的光滑外力在相对 $L^1_tH^s_x$ 拓扑中稠密当且仅当 $s<1/2$，在相对 $L^2_tH^s_x$ 拓扑中稠密当且仅当 $s<-1/2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前紧致、光滑受迫的 Navier–Stokes 爆破构造从局部或周期设定推广到全空间 $\mathbb R^3$，并刻画在何种时空拓扑下爆破外力具有稠密性，从而厘清外力空间、初值空间与数值误差正则性之间的区别。

Method: 采用紧致无散度地移除正则背景项、再精确插入单个全空间奇异包的方法；辅以独立的临界估计证明非稠密性；对低空间频率作显式控制，不使用周期均值或 Poincaré 不等式。

Result: 对任意固定正黏度与期限，光滑爆破外力在相对 $L^1_tH^s_x$ 中稠密当且仅当 $s<1/2$，在相对 $L^2_tH^s_x$ 中稠密当且仅当 $s<-1/2$；正向结果保持每个固定的 $H^\infty_\sigma(\mathbb R^3)$ 初速度。结论适用于时空紧支外力及 Clay 表述中的快速衰减数据类，并得到通常完备能量—外力空间中的稠密性、正则轨道的强能量—耗散逼近，以及在一给定有限全空间网格族上单元观测的精确相等。

Conclusion: 爆破型外力在临界指数两侧表现出稠密性阈值，且该结论可推广至全空间、紧支外力与 Clay 快速衰减数据类；全程区分初值空间、外力空间与数值误差正则性，且不主张固定外力失稳、弱存在性丧失或新的形式化验证。

Abstract: We derive whole-space distribution results from the established compact, smoothly forced Navier--Stokes blowup construction of OpenAI. For every fixed positive viscosity and deadline, smooth forces causing classical breakdown from rest on $\mathbb R^3$ are dense in the relative $L^1_tH^s_x$ topology exactly when $s<1/2$, and in the relative $L^2_tH^s_x$ topology exactly when $s<-1/2$. The positive results preserve every fixed initial velocity in $H^\infty_σ(\R^3)$. They follow from a compact divergence-free removal of a regular background, followed by exact insertion of a single whole-space singular packet. Separate critical estimates prove non-density; low spatial frequencies are controlled explicitly, without a periodic mean or a Poincaré inequality. The conclusions hold for spacetime-compact forces and for the rapidly decaying data class in the Clay formulation. We also obtain density in the usual completed energy-force spaces, strong energy-and-dissipation approximation of regular trajectories, and exact equality of cell observations on a prescribed finite family of whole-space grids. Initial-state spaces, forcing spaces and numerical error regularity are distinguished throughout. No claim of fixed-force instability, loss of weak existence or new formal verification is made.

</details>


### [184] [The Schrödinger equation with fluctuating nonlinearity in the energy space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10417)
*Max Sauerbrey,Joris van Winden*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带非线性Stratonovich噪声的非线性Schrödinger方程，在能量空间$H^1$中建立局部适定性、爆破替代及全局适定性，改进了指数范围与噪声空间正则性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人对随机非线性Schrödinger方程的结果在非线性指数$p,q$的范围以及噪声的空间正则性假设上存在限制，本文旨在放宽这些限制，扩大适定性结果的适用范围。

Method: 结合文献[Potential Anal. 41 (2014)]中的随机Strichartz估计与文献[Ann. Inst. H. Poincaré Phys. Théor. 46 (1987)]的方法，处理非线性Stratonovich噪声$\mathrm{d}u = i[\Delta u + \lambda|u|^{p-1}u]\mathrm{d}t + i|u|^{(q-1)/2}u\circ \mathrm{d}W$。

Result: 对所有能量次临界非线性$p,q\in[1,1+4/(d-2)_+)$得到局部适定性与相应的爆破替代；对线性乘性噪声$q=1$、实值噪声$W$及散焦非线性$\lambda\le0$，通过能量界验证爆破条件，得到全局适定性；当$p,q\in[1,1+4/d)$（质量次临界）时，给出涉及$L^2$范数的改进爆破判据，并利用实值$W$的质量守恒亦得到全局适定性。

Conclusion: 相比于已有的随机非线性Schrödinger方程结果，本文改进了指数$p,q$的范围以及噪声的空间正则性假设，并针对线性乘性噪声与质量次临界情形分别证明了全局适定性。

Abstract: We study nonlinear Schrödinger equations with nonlinear Stratonovich noise
  \begin{equation*}
  \mathrm{d} u\,=\, i\bigl[ Δu \,+\, λ|u|^{p-1}u\bigr] \, \mathrm{d} t \,+\,i|u|^{(q-1)/2}u\circ \mathrm{d} {W},
  \end{equation*} in their energy space $H^1(\mathbb R^d;\mathbb C)$. By combining the stochastic Strichartz estimates derived in [Potential Anal. 41 (2014), pp.\ 269--315] with the approach from [Ann.\ Inst.\ H.\ Poincaré Phys.\ Théor.\ 46 (1987), pp.\ 113--129] we obtain local well-posedness for all energy-subcritical nonlinearities $p,q\in [1, 1+4/(d-2)_+)$ together with a corresponding blow-up alternative. For a linear multiplicative noise $q=1$, a real-valued noise $W$ and a defocusing nonlinearity $λ\le 0$, we check this blow-up condition using a bound on the energy, resulting in the global well-posedness of the equation. If both nonlinearities are mass-subcritical, i.e., $p,q\in [1, 1+4/d)$, we provide an improved blow-up criterion involving the $L^2(\mathbb R^d;\mathbb C)$-norm. Using the conservation of mass for real-valued $W$, we obtain global well-posedness also in this case. Compared to previous results on stochastic nonlinear Schrödinger equations, we thereby improve the range of exponents $p$ and $q$ and the spatial regularity assumption on the noise.

</details>


### [185] [On the $\mathrm{L}^p$-theory of the generalized Stokes operator with rough coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10427)
*Luca Haardt*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立具界可测系数的广义Stokes算子的L^p映射性质，覆盖粗糙系数椭圆系统已知的尖锐指标范围，并发展了一套刻画超压缩性、非局部衰减估计与一致L^p界之间关系的抽象框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙系数椭圆系统下L^p理论的最优指标范围已被知晓，但具界可测系数的广义Stokes算子的相应L^p映射性质尚待建立，且其中超压缩性、非局部衰减与一致L^p界之间的内在联系缺乏统一解释框架。

Method: 建立广义Stokes算子的L^p映射性质，包括半群及其梯度的L^p估计、H^∞-泛函演算以及L^q-L^p光滑化估计；作为关键工具，构建一个抽象框架，用以刻画超压缩性、非局部衰减估计与一致L^p界之间的关系。

Result: 在粗糙系数椭圆系统已知尖锐的完整指标范围内，得到了广义Stokes算子半群、其梯度与H^∞-泛函演算的L^p估计，以及L^q-L^p光滑化估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将Stokes算子的L^p理论推广至具界可测系数的尖锐范围，并提出的抽象框架为超压缩性、非局部衰减与一致L^p界的关系提供了统一处理手段。

Abstract: In this article, we establish $\mathrm{L}^p$-mapping properties for the generalized Stokes operator with bounded measurable coefficients on the full range of exponents that is known to be sharp for elliptic systems with rough coefficients. These include $\mathrm{L}^p$-estimates for the corresponding semigroup, its gradient and the $\mathrm{H}^\infty$-calculus, as well as $\mathrm{L}^q$-$\mathrm{L}^p$ smoothing estimates. As a key ingredient, we develop an abstract framework that captures the relationship between hypercontractivity, non-local decay estimates and uniform $Ł^p$-bounds.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Distinguishability Transitions from Global Quantum Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09296)
*Catherine McCarthy,Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何用少量测量结果区分多个随机量子态，发现贝叶斯分类器区分k个候选态所需m次测量的能力存在由x=m/ln k控制的相变，并推导出阈值x_c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般时间演化生成的量子态在局部测量下表现为无特征性，即局部测量对所有态返回相同随机结果；然而计算基下的全局快照却能有效区分量子态，这一反差促使作者探讨有限测量资源下区分众多随机量子态的可行性。

Method: 采用贝叶斯分类器对多个随机量子态进行判别，使用少量测量结果，并以变量x=m/ln k刻画所需测量次数m与候选态数目k的关系；进一步将分析推广到接近反集中起始的低深度电路、含噪声电路以及相位随机态。

Result: 识别出贝叶斯分类器区分k个候选态能力上的相变，该相变由x=m/ln k控制，并推导出相应的阈值x_c；同时验证该结论在低深度电路、噪声电路和相位随机态情形下的适用性。

Conclusion: 全局计算基测量在区分随机量子态方面具有高效性，贝叶斯分类能力存在明确的阈值相变，为有限测量资源下的量子态判别提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: Quantum states generated by generic time evolution are locally featureless: local measurements return random outcomes that are identical for all states. However, global snapshots in the computational basis are surprisingly effective at distinguishing between quantum states. We explore the ability of Bayesian classifiers to discriminate between many random quantum states using a small number of measurement outcomes. We identify a phase transition in the ability of the classifier to distinguish between $k$ candidate states with $m$ shots controlled by the variable $x = m / \ln k$, and derive the threshold value $x_c$. We extend our results to low-depth circuits near the onset of anti-concentration, as well as to noisy circuits and phase-random states.

</details>


### [187] [Transient and universal regimes in quantum reaction-transport kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09305)
*Hossein Hosseinabadi,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 量子反应-输运系统中，A+A→∅的动力学在低于上临界维度D_c=2时受涨落支配，一维呈输运受限标度n~t^{-1/2}，二维出现对数修正，高于二维恢复平均场标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为量子反应-输运系统的弛豫由反应时标与输运时标的相对大小决定，分为反应受限或输运受限。作者希望检验这一预期在低维量子反应系统中是否成立，特别是量子涨落对渐近动力学的修正作用。

Method: 研究量子双分子湮灭过程A+A→∅，通过分析反应过程在上临界维度D_c=2以下的奇异涨落修正，考察不同维度下端正动力学行为，并识别量子Zeno标度及有效弹性碰撞的涌现机制。

Result: 在D<2时涨落主导渐近动力学；一维中平均场弛豫变为瞬态，系统进入输运受限区，n~t^{-1/2}，受量子Zeno标度控制，即使极弱损耗也成立。D=2时动力学出现对数修正；D>2时渐近恢复平均场标度。在临界维度及以下，平均场行为可在参数化长的渡越时间内持续。同时涨落产生有效弹性碰撞，在D>1时参数化快于损耗，使系统趋向准静态热态。

Conclusion: 低维量子反应-输运系统的弛豫分类预期失效，涨落主导渐近动力学并改变标度区；量子Zeno标度与有效弹性碰撞是决定输运受限区和准静态热化的关键机制。

Abstract: Quantum reaction-transport systems consist of coherently propagating particles that irreversibly react upon encounter. Their relaxation is commonly classified as reaction-limited or transport-limited, depending on the relative timescales of reaction and particle transport. We show that this expectation fails in low dimensions by studying the quantum binary annihilation, $A+A \to \emptyset$, where reaction processes acquire singular fluctuation corrections below the upper critical dimension $D_c=2$. Consequently, fluctuations dominate the asymptotic kinetics for $D<2$. In one dimension, they render mean-field relaxation transient and drive the system toward a transport-limited regime with $n\sim t^{-1/2}$, governed by a quantum-Zeno scale even for arbitrarily weak loss. At $D=2$, the kinetics acquires logarithmic corrections, whereas above two dimensions mean-field scaling is asymptotically restored. At and below the upper critical dimension, mean-field behavior can nevertheless persist over parametrically long crossover times before the asymptotic fluctuation-dominated regime emerges. We also show that the same fluctuations generate effective elastic collisions despite the absence of microscopic coherent interactions. In $D>1$, these collisions redistribute momentum populations and are parametrically faster than losses in the transport-limited regime, allowing the system to approach a quasi-stationary thermal state.

</details>


### [188] [Exact asymptotic equivalence between precisions of record-times and integrated currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09479)
*Alberto Garilli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明了连续时间马尔可夫跳跃过程中积分电流涨落与电流记录事件时间统计之间的精确等价关系：电流的Fano因子恰好等于记录间等待时间的变异系数平方，并通过Narayana数刻画其母函数，将结果从单环网络推广到任意有限状态图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于电流涨落与记录事件时间统计之间联系的结果仅限于单环网络，缺乏对一般有限状态图的统一描述。作者希望揭示电流精度（涨落）的结构性起源，并建立适用于任意网络的普适等价关系。

Method: 在纯概率框架内，利用记录事件将系统重置到固定状态这一性质，推出记录间时间具有更新（renewal）结构；进一步用Narayana数给出记录间时间母函数的精确组合表示，从而建立积分电流Fano因子与记录间等待时间变异系数平方的精确等价。

Result: 在最小假设下，积分电流的Fano因子精确等于记录间等待时间的变异系数平方；该等价关系对任意有限状态图成立，并给出电流涨落以记录时间可观测量表示的统一形式，推广了单环网络的结果。

Conclusion: 电流涨落与记录事件时间统计之间的等价是普适的，其根源在于记录间时间的更新结构及Narayana数控制的组合组织，从而以首达类事件统计刻画了电流精度的结构起源。

Abstract: We establish an exact equivalence between the fluctuations of integrated currents and the temporal statistics of current record events in continuous-time Markov jump processes. Specifically, we show that, under minimal assumptions, the Fano factor of an integrated current coincides exactly with the squared coefficient of variation of the inter-record waiting times. This result is obtained by showing that inter-record times induce a renewal structure, since each record event resets the system to a fixed state, and that their generating function can be expressed exactly in terms of a combinatorial organization governed by Narayana numbers. The equivalence extends previous results obtained for unicyclic networks to arbitrary finite-state graphs and provides a unified representation of current fluctuations in terms of record-time observables. The results are derived within a purely probabilistic framework and clarify the structural origin of current precision in terms of first-passage-like event statistics.

</details>


### [189] [Gap-controlled thermalization in a SSH model version of the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09498)
*José A. Aké,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究二聚化FPUT链（经典SSH类模型），发现当二聚化强度达到最大值一半时出现尖锐的隔离阈值，该阈值由首个umklapp过程开启决定；低于阈值时三波声-声-光散射在约1e4周期尺度激活光学支，高于阈值时两支保持动力学隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典非谐晶格中，带隙会重塑驱动非线性模式混合的共振结构，但光谱带隙究竟阻碍还是促进长时间热化尚不清楚。作者旨在厘清声学-光学带隙对非线性能量输运与热化过程的调控作用。

Method: 采用交替弹簧常数的二聚化Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou链（经典SSH模型类比），引入α型（立方）非线性；通过长时间辛（symplectic）数值模拟追踪各模态能量与谱熵，并考察不同边界条件的影响。

Result: 当二聚化强度达到其最大值的一半时出现尖锐的隔离阈值，对应首个umplapp（Umklapp）过程开启，即两个带边界声学声子可融合为一个带中心光学声子。阈值以下，三波声-声-光散射在约1e4个振荡周期的时间尺度上激活光学支；阈值以上，两支保持动力学隔离。此外，边界条件会改变该图像，在特定模式激发下产生长寿命的sticky态。

Conclusion: 声子带隙可作为动量选择性的可调滤波器，用于控制非线性能量输运，为二聚化或拓扑带隙非线性晶格中的热化调控提供了通用途径。

Abstract: In classical anharmonic lattices, the resonance structure driving nonlinear mode mixing is reshaped by band gaps. However, it remains unclear whether a spectral gap hinders or promotes long-time thermalization. Here we study a dimerized Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chain - a classical SSH model analogue with alternating spring constants - and quantify how the acoustic-optical gap controls relaxation under alpha-type (cubic) nonlinearity. Tracking modal energies and spectral entropy via long-time symplectic simulations, we find a sharp isolation threshold when the dimerization strength reaches half its maximum value, set by the onset of the first umklapp process that allows two zone-boundary acoustic phonons to fuse into a zone-center optical phonon; below this threshold, three-wave acoustic-acoustic-optical scattering activates the optical branch on timescales of order 1e4 oscillation periods, while above it the bands remain dynamically isolated. We further show that boundary conditions reshape this picture, producing long-lived sticky states for specific mode excitations. These results establish the phononic gap as a tunable, momentum-selective filter for nonlinear energy transport, suggesting a general route to controlling thermalization in dimerized or topologically gapped nonlinear lattices.

</details>


### [190] [Topological instability and reentrant crystallization in active solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09500)
*Zory Davoyan,Aditya Jha,Anton Souslov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将KTHNY二维缺陷介导熔化理论推广至具有非互易（奇）弹性和Cosserat（微极）耦合的活性固体，发现除活性导致的熔化温度偏移外，还存在一个由非互易性阈值触发的全新"拓扑不稳定性"熔化机制，并预测了高温下的重入结晶现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典KTHNY理论描述了平衡态二维固体通过缺陷解绑实现的熔化。然而活性固体兼具非互易（奇）弹性与Cosserat（微极）耦合，其熔化行为是否仍遵循KTHNY图像尚不清楚，需要建立相应的理论框架。

Method: 将KTHNY缺陷介导熔化理论推广到同时具有奇弹性和Cosserat耦合的活性固体；结合场论分析与数值模拟，以缺陷对逸度（fugacity）为参量刻画该转变。

Result: 在微扰小的非互易性下，熔化仍通过缺陷解绑进行，但熔化温度因活性而移动；当非互易性达到阈值时，出现一种定性的新熔化机制——"拓扑不稳定性"，即在任意温度下通过缺陷对的增殖实现熔化，表现为零温转变。更高活性下出现重入结晶，即使温度高于平衡晶体的熔化温度，准长程有序仍能存活。

Conclusion: 活性固体是拓扑不稳定性的一个具体实现，但核心能驱动的缺陷增殖可能是一类普遍且尚未被探索的二维熔化路径。

Abstract: We extend the KTHNY theory of defect-mediated melting in two dimensions to the case of active solids exhibiting both non-reciprocal (odd) elasticity and Cosserat (micropolar) coupling. For perturbatively small values of non-reciprocity, melting still proceeds via defect unbinding, but the melting temperature shifts due to activity. However, at a threshold value of non-reciprocity, we discover a qualitatively distinct mechanism for 2D melting, which we call topological instability. This melting occurs through the proliferation of defect pairs at any temperature. We use a combination of field theory and simulations to characterize this zero-temperature transition in terms of the defect-pair fugacity. Surprisingly, for higher activity values, we find reentrant crystallization, where the quasi-long-range order survives even at temperatures for which the equilibrium crystal would melt. Although active solids present one realization of this topological instability, we envision core-energy-driven defect proliferation as a generic and unexplored route for two-dimensional melting.

</details>


### [191] [The Role of Concentration in Determining NaCl Nucleation Mechanism: A Story of Pathways Coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09518)
*Porhouy Minh,Sapna Sarupria*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用无偏路径采样方法 ∞RETIS 研究 NaCl 水溶液在 S=3.2–3.8 下的成核机制，发现不同过饱和度受同一反应坐标主导，但反应路径性质随过饱和度连续变化：低浓度近似一步成核，高浓度下非晶聚集与结晶逐渐解耦，两类路径共存于单一宽反应通道内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NaCl 水溶液成核中非晶离子聚集的作用程度，以及类两步机制出现的过饱和度区间长期存在争议，本文旨在无先验机制假设下厘清其微观路径。

Method: 在 12、13、14 mol/kg（S = 3.2、3.5、3.8）条件下，采用 ∞RETIS 无偏路径采样，结合反应坐标分析、联合与条件概率分布、扩散张量分析及条件自由能景观。

Result: 所有浓度均由同一反应坐标控制，说明核生长与结构有序化是跨越势垒的关键；但反应轨迹性质随过饱和度改变。低浓度下团簇尺寸增长与有序化紧密耦合，表现为类一步机制；随浓度升高，非晶聚集与结晶逐渐解耦，先发生显著非晶生长再结晶的路径概率增大。这些路径共存于单一宽反应通道，而非通过不同的亚稳中间态。

Conclusion: 研究调和了关于 NaCl 成核机制的矛盾报道，支持将成核视为一组相对概率随过饱和度连续变化的竞争反应路径；并表明在经典成核框架内即可出现显著的机制多样性，无需引入不同的非经典描述。

Abstract: Deciphering the microscopic details of crystal nucleation remains a key open problem in chemical physics. For aqueous sodium chloride (NaCl), the extent to which nucleation involves amorphous ion aggregation, and the supersaturation regime in which two-step-like behavior emerges, remains actively debated. In this work, we examine NaCl nucleation at 12, 13, and 14 mol/kg ($S = 3.2, 3.5, 3.8$) using unbiased path sampling with $\infty$RETIS. Through a combination of reaction coordinate analysis, joint and conditional probability distributions, diffusion tensor analysis, and conditional free energy landscapes, we identify nucleation pathways without a priori mechanistic assumptions. While all concentrations are governed by the same reaction coordinate, indicating that nucleus growth and structural ordering are central to crossing the barrier, the nature of reactive trajectories changes with supersaturation. At lower concentrations, nucleation mainly occurs via tightly coupled increases in cluster size and ordering, compatible with a one-step-like mechanism. As concentration increases, amorphous aggregation becomes increasingly decoupled from crystallization, and pathways involving substantial amorphous growth prior to crystallization become progressively more probable. Importantly, these pathways coexist within a single broad reaction channel rather than proceeding through distinct metastable intermediate states. Together, these results reconcile earlier, contradictory reports on NaCl nucleation mechanisms and support a view of nucleation as an ensemble of competing reactive pathways whose relative probabilities vary continuously with supersaturation. More broadly, our findings illustrate that substantial mechanistic diversity can emerge within a classical nucleation framework, without requiring distinct non-classical descriptions.

</details>


### [192] [Rare-event sampling for stochastic dynamics in network systems using cluster updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16171)
*Jiazheng Sun,James Moody,Thomas Barthel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出条件路径蒙特卡洛（CPMC）方法，用于高效模拟复杂网络中满足宏观边界条件的稀有随机演化事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂网络中的稀有宏观事件（如通信网络级联失效、疫情暴发、舆论突变）常由局部稀有随机过程与瓶颈效应共同引发。传统正向模拟（Gillespie）拒绝率过高，而分裂法、过渡路径采样等稀有事件技术在异质网络上易出现动力学捕获、路径退化、谱系关联或临界慢化等问题。

Method: 受平衡态凝聚态物理中的loop算法启发，提出条件路径蒙特卡洛（CPMC）。该方法在时空气团（spacetime clusters）上进行非局域更新且无拒绝，生成严格满足目标宏观边界条件（如大规模网络失效发生）的轨迹马尔可夫链。

Result: 在亲缘网络上的SIS动力学中，对稀有大规模疫情暴发进行了简单风险因子分析，展示了该框架的潜力。

Conclusion: CPMC为复杂网络中稀有宏观事件的模拟提供了有效框架，可克服现有方法的多种局限，并具备应用于风险分析的潜力。

Abstract: Understanding the stochastic evolution in complex networks is a central challenge across physics, biology, engineering, social science, and finance. The most consequential macroscopic events, like cascading failures in communication networks, widespread epidemic outbreaks, and rapid shifts in societal opinions, often emerge from a confluence of rare, localized stochastic processes and need to pass certain bottlenecks. Standard forward-time simulation algorithms like the Gillespie method are inefficient for the investigation of such phenomena due to catastrophic rejection rates. Advanced rare-event techniques like splitting methods and transition-path sampling often suffer from kinetic trapping, path degeneracy, genealogical correlations, or critical slowing down when applied to complex heterogeneous networks. We propose to overcome this challenge by establishing a novel technique called conditional-path Monte Carlo (CPMC), inspired by loop algorithms from equilibrium condensed-matter physics. By employing non-local updates on spacetime clusters without rejections, CPMC generates a Markov chain of trajectories that all strictly respect the targeted macroscopic boundary conditions like the occurrence of a massive network failure. We demonstrate the framework's potential by performing a simple risk factor analysis for rare large-scale epidemic outbreaks in SIS dynamics on kinship networks.

</details>


### [193] [The Arithmetic of Spectra: Factorization, Statistics, and Symmetric Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10391)
*A. Chaudhary*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究非相互作用量子系统单粒子谱的因子分解及其对多粒子统计的影响，用"谱字母表"将张量分解转化为字母表的乘法分解，并借助对称函数与λ-环恒等式提升为典则Bose/Fermi配分函数，给出一个统一的组合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相互作用量子系统的单粒子谱可发生张量分解，而多粒子统计（Bose/Fermi）对谱结构敏感。现有处理缺乏统一、组合化的语言来刻画哪些谱分解是允许的、反对称化如何分配等问题，因此需要一个将谱分解与多粒子统计联系起来的统一框架。

Method: 将单粒子配分函数表示为"谱字母表"（spectral alphabet），使张量分解对应字母表的乘法分解；再利用标准对称函数与λ-环恒等式，将字母表分解提升为典则的Bose与Fermi配分函数；进而分析乘积谱如何由不同分解产生、反对称化在奇数个因子上的分配，以及与固定谱相容的张量分解条件。

Result: 建立了谱分解与多粒子统计之间的对应：张量分解↔字母表乘法分解；给出了乘积谱的不同来源；说明了反对称化可跨奇数个因子分配；刻画了与给定固定谱相容的张量分解集合。

Conclusion: 该文为典则Bose与Fermi配分函数提供了一个整合的组合框架，将谱因子分解问题纳入对称函数与λ-环的理论体系，统一处理多粒子统计中的分解与反对称化问题。

Abstract: We study factorizations of the single-particle spectrum of non-interacting quantum systems and their consequences for many-particle statistics. Representing the single-particle partition function by a spectral alphabet, tensor factorizations become multiplicative factorizations of the alphabet, which can be lifted to canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions using standard symmetric-function and $λ$-ring identities. We show how product spectra arise from different factorizations, how antisymmetrization can be assigned across an odd number of factors, and which tensor factorizations are compatible with a fixed spectrum. The paper therefore studies spectral factorization and provides a consolidated combinatorial framework for canonical Bose and Fermi partition functions.

</details>


### [194] [Energy Transport Structure and Fluctuation Theorem in Nonreciprocal Harmonic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09593)
*Ziwei Cheng,Geng Li,Zhanchun Tu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为一维非对称耦合谐振子链建立了精确的输运结构，揭示了热流方向由温度偏置与等效方向不对称性的竞争决定，可实现冷到热的逆向热输运，并给出了包含非互易功率流的广义稳态能量平衡及满足Gallavotti-Cohen对称性的涨落框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用打破了前后向响应的对称性，从根本上改变能量输运。现有研究对非互易热输运的能量学结构与涨落规律尚缺乏统一、精确的理论刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白，并探索方向性相互作用能否作为主动资源调控非平衡能量流。

Method: 研究对象为一维谐振子链，其最近邻耦合为非对称（非互易）。采用格林函数方法求解精确输运结构；在稳态层面分析温度偏置与等效方向不对称性的竞争；在涨落层面，将两个热库的热交换视为相关联但不同的随机流，计算其联合标度累积生成函数并检验其对称性。

Result: 热流方向不由温度偏置单独决定，而取决于其与等效方向不对称性的竞争，该竞争可反转热流方向，实现冷到热的输运。非互易性引入了一个与相互作用反对称扇区相关的额外功率通道，从而给出包含两个热库热流与一个非互易功率流的广义稳态能量平衡。两个热库的热交换构成相关联但不同的随机流，其联合标度累积生成函数满足精确的Gallavotti-Cohen对称性。
