Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的能量学与涨落框架来描述非互易热输运，表明方向性相互作用可作为控制非平衡能量流的主动资源，为非互易热管理与能量调控提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal interactions fundamentally alter energy transport by breaking the symmetry between forward and backward responses. Here, we uncover an exact transport structure for a one-dimensional harmonic chain with asymmetric nearest-neighbor couplings. Using a Green-function approach, we demonstrate that the direction of heat transport is determined not solely by the temperature bias, but by its competition with an effective directional asymmetry. This competition can reverse the direction of heat flow, enabling cold-to-hot transport. We further show that nonreciprocity introduces an additional power channel associated with the antisymmetric sector of the interaction, leading to a generalized steady-state energy balance involving two reservoir heat currents and a nonreciprocal power current. At the fluctuation level, the heat exchanges with the two reservoirs constitute correlated yet distinct stochastic currents, whose joint scaled cumulant generating function obeys an exact Gallavotti-Cohen symmetry. Together, these results establish a unified energetic and fluctuation framework for nonreciprocal heat transport and demonstrate that directional interactions can serve as an active resource for controlling nonequilibrium energy flows.

</details>


### [195] [Protected domains and the cost of cooperation on weighted networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10463)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究有限接触结构上的捐赠博弈中合作（集体竞争者）的演化，结合精确涨落响应与快构型消去，识别"受保护区域"的形成与竞争为控制过程。给出弱选择阈值：四环 b/c=3、五点路径 b/c=7、六点及以上固定路径 b/c=1；并刻画阈值为 1+ε 时所需的最小接触对比度 656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)]、中性吸收时间量级 ε^{-1} 及其权衡与设计容差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个问题：哪些有限接触结构能维持这种"集体竞争者"（合作行为），以及其优势需要付出什么代价。核心张力在于：一个有成本的遗传性状只有通过后代营造有利局部环境才能传播，而不同接触结构对这一过程的支撑能力差异巨大。

Method: 设定个体进行捐赠博弈，付出成本 c 带来收益 b。互惠加权接触决定平均收益；受体按收益选择复制对象，源个体则按收益全局选择。以"均匀引入的合作者比互补实验中的背叛者更常占优"作为合作被偏好的判据。运用精确涨落响应与快构型消去，识别受保护区域的形成与竞争为控制过程；通过接触比之间的多项式关系控制无约束优化，并导出渐近精确反射对称性。

Result: 锐利的弱选择阈值下确界为：四环上 b/c=3，五点路径上 b/c=7，六点及以上的任意固定路径上 b/c=1。在六点路径上，全局界迫使近势垒设计落入该结构层级。达到阈值 1+ε 需要最小接触对比度 656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)] 与量级为 ε^{-1} 的中性吸收时间，并确定二者权衡及相应设计容差；有限选择消耗同样的余量。在受控小选择窗口内，当对比度超过其精确最小值约 ε^{-1} 量级时，最大固定优势为 ε^{5/2} 量级。

Conclusion: 改善入侵阈值往往要求接触异质性、精度与观察时间同步增加；即更低的合作门槛以更高的接触对比度、设计容差要求和更长的中性吸收时间为代价，二者之间存在可量化的权衡关系。

Abstract: A costly inherited trait can spread when its descendants create a favorable local environment. We ask which finite contact structures sustain such a collective competitor and what their advantage costs. Individuals play a donation game, conferring benefit $b$ at cost $c$. Reciprocal weighted contacts determine averaged payoffs and copying recipients, while sources are chosen globally according to payoff. Cooperation is favored when a uniformly introduced cooperator takes over more often than a defector in the complementary experiment. Exact fluctuation response and elimination of fast configurations identify the formation and competition of protected domains as the controlling processes. The sharp weak-selection threshold infima are $b/c=3$ on the four-cycle, $7$ on the five-site path, and $1$ on every fixed path with at least six sites. On the six-site path, a global bound forces near-barrier designs into this domain hierarchy. Polynomial relations among the contact ratios then control unrestricted optimization and imply eventual exact reflection symmetry. Reaching threshold $1+ε$ requires minimum contact contrast $656ε^{-2}[1+O(ε)]$ and neutral absorption time of order $ε^{-1}$. We determine their tradeoff and the associated design tolerance. Finite selection consumes the same margin. Within the controlled small-selection window, the maximal fixation advantage is of order $ε^{5/2}$ when the contrast exceeds its exact minimum by order $ε^{-1}$. Thus an improving invasion threshold can require increasing contact heterogeneity, precision and observation time.

</details>


### [196] [Renewal process's guide to fractional Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09620)
*Hong Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出利用碰撞更新过程（任意分布等待时间）推导玻尔兹曼方程，并证明指数等待时间对应经典Navier-Stokes方程、幂律等待时间对应分数阶Navier-Stokes方程，从而给出求解这两类方程的随机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes方程尚未解决，是流体力学核心难题，也是千禧年难题之一。Hilbert曾提出通过建立微观动力学与宏观连续方程之间联系来攻克此问题，其关键桥梁是玻尔兹曼方程的推导与概率论。

Method: 采用具有任意分布等待时间的碰撞更新过程（collision renewal process），推导描述粒子速度与位移概率随时间演化的玻尔兹曼方程。

Result: 证明了具有指数碰撞等待时间的更新过程等价于经典Navier-Stokes方程；具有幂律等待时间的更新过程等价于分数阶Navier-Stokes方程。

Conclusion: 由于任意分布等待时间的碰撞更新过程是随机过程，且易于通过轨迹的随机模拟获得相应解，因此实际上找到了一种求解经典与分数阶Navier-Stokes方程的随机方法。

Abstract: The Navier-Stokes equations, which remain unsolved, are crucial equations in fluid mechanics. Discovering the solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations is one of the challenging Millennium problems. In 1900, Hilbert proposed a potential approach to tackle this problem by establishing the relationship between microscopic dynamics and the macroscopic continuum equations. The key bridge is the derivation of Boltzmann equation and the theory of probability. In this paper, we shall use the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times to derive the Boltzmann equation for the time evolution of the probability of the velocity and the displacement of the particle, based on which we prove that the renewal process with exponential collision waiting time is equivalent to the classical Navier-Stokes equations, and that with power-law waiting time is equivalent to the fractional Navier-Stokes equations. Since the collision renewal process with arbitrarily distributed waiting times is a random process and is easy to perform the stochastic simulations of trajectories to obtain the corresponding solution, we actually find a stochastic approach to solve the classical and fractional Navier-Stokes equations.

</details>


### [197] [Intermittent continuous-time random walks under renewal reset mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09738)
*Guohua Li,Hong Zhang,Yuexiong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究随机重置下的间歇性连续时间随机游走（CTRW）模型，以跳跃与重置等待时间的较小值作为更新时刻，推导控制方程与Montroll-Weiss方程，证明非平衡稳态存在性，并分析均方位移与平均首达时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置作为一种高效搜索策略在复杂无序环境中受到广泛关注，作者希望基于跳跃与重置之间的竞争机制刻画更一般的间歇性搜索过程。

Method: 以跳跃和重置等待时间的较小者作为更新间隔，构建间歇性CTRW模型（跳跃与重置等待时间可服从任意分布），推导更新重置与马尔可夫重置下的控制方程及Montroll-Weiss方程；针对指数与幂律等待时间分布证明非平衡稳态；对指数与高斯跳跃步长计算均方位移的单调性与渐近稳定性，并通过首达时间量化搜索效率。

Result: 当跳跃与重置等待时间服从任意指数或幂律分布时，更新重置机制下存在非平衡稳态；在指数跳跃+指数重置、幂律跳跃+指数重置、指数跳跃+幂律重置三类情形下，平均首达时间（MFAT）有限；而当跳跃与重置等待时间均服从幂律分布时，MFAT发散。

Conclusion: 基于竞争机制的间歇性CTRW模型可应用于多种物理场景，如动物觅食失败后返回巢穴的策略，以及智能机器人长时间运行后返回充电点的工作规划。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting as a practical and efficient search strategy in complex and disordered environments has long been a topic of interest to researchers. Based on the competition between jumping and resetting, this article proposes and investigates intermittent continuous-time random walks (CTRWs) under stochastic resetting, using the smaller waiting time for jump and reset as the renewal time, where the waiting times for both jump and reset can have arbitrary distributions. After each renewal event, the system will proceed with new waiting times for jump and reset regardless of their previous histories. We study the governing equation and Montroll-Weiss equation with renewal resetting, as well as the Markovian resetting for intermittent CTRWs. We prove the existence of non-equilibrium stationary states within the renewal reset mechanism when the jump and reset waiting times follow any exponential and power law distributions. For exponential and Gaussian distributed jump lengths, we examine the mean square displacements (MSDs) of particles to determine their monotonicity and asymptotic stability. Moreover, we calculate the first-arrival time to quantify search efficiency, and validate the intermittent CTRWs under renewal resetting lead to a finite mean first-arrival time (MFAT) to any fixed position for exponential jump and reset waiting time distributions (WTDs), power-law jump and exponential reset WTDs, as well as exponential jump and power-law reset WTDs. However, the MFAT diverges for power-law jump and reset WTDs. The intermittent CTRW model, which is based on the competition mechanism, can be applied to many physical scenarios, such as the foraging strategy of animals that return to their nests after an unsuccessful foraging attempt, or the work planning of intelligent robots that return to energy replenishment points after prolonged operation.

</details>


### [198] [Quantifying cell shape and density fluctuations in epithelial tissue in vivo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09874)
*Jake Turley,Helen Weavers,Paul Martin,Isaac V. Chenchiah,Tanniemola B. Liverpool*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论框架，将上皮细胞建模为可变尺寸与长宽比的软椭球体，通过粗粒化得到连续随机微分方程，导出时空关联函数，并与果蝇蛹翅实验数据拟合，从而提取细胞主动形变与组织有效刚度的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织通常具有细胞形状与尺寸的异质性，局部偏离平均形状与尺寸的涨落可跨组织传播，可能揭示组织有效"刚度"或刚性等特征，但缺乏刻画这类涨落动力学的理论框架。

Method: 将细胞视为相互作用、尺寸与长宽比可变的软椭球体；对模型进行粗粒化，得到一组连续随机微分方程，并据其推导时空关联函数；将关联函数与果蝇蛹翅发育过程的实验数据进行拟合。

Result: 理论推导的时空关联函数能够较好地拟合果蝇蛹翅发育的实验数据，并可从关联函数中确定表征细胞主动形状变化与组织有效"刚度"的关键参数。

Conclusion: 该框架可同时刻画上皮细胞形状的平均行为与涨落模式，为从涨落中定量提取组织的活性与力学特性提供途径。

Abstract: Controlling changes in cell shape are crucial for many biological processes, such as tissue development and wound healing. Tissues typically are heterogeneous with a variety of cell shapes and sizes. Of particular interest are local deviations from an average cell shape and size. These fluctuations may extend and transmit across tissues, potentially offering valuable insights into tissue characteristics such as variations in effective "stiffness" or rigidity. In this study, we present a theoretical framework that captures the dynamics of epithelial cell shapes within tissue, incorporating both their average behaviour and fluctuation patterns. We model cells as interacting soft ellipsoids of varying size and aspect ratio. Coarse-graining our model, we obtain a set of continuum stochastic differential equations from which we derive spatial-temporal correlation functions. These correlation functions fit with our experimental data from the developmental process of the \textit{Drosophila} pupal wing. From the correlation functions, critical parameters representing active cell shape changes and effective tissue "stiffness" can be determined.

</details>


### [199] [Efficient protocol for the Markovian Mpemba effect in $N$-level systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09988)
*David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文给出三能级系统马尔可夫姆潘巴效应的充要条件并阐明物理机制，进而提出在N能级系统中高效确定相关参数的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姆潘巴效应是一种违反直觉的弛豫现象：热化时间并不随距平衡态的距离单调变化，初始温差更大的系统反而可能先热化。该效应较为罕见，难以寻找能产生它的参数，从而阻碍了实验设计与应用开发。

Method: (1) 在三能级系统中推导马尔可夫姆潘巴效应成立的充要条件；(2) 分析并解释其背后的物理机制；(3) 基于对三能级情形的理解，设计一种高效算法，用于在N能级系统中确定产生该效应所需的参数。

Result: 获得了三能级系统马尔可夫姆潘巴效应的充要条件，并揭示了其物理机制；所提算法能够高效地确定N能级系统中的相关参数。

Conclusion: 该协议有望推动姆潘巴效应实验在大尺度系统中的实现及在相关应用中的落地。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect is an anomalous phenomenon in which the time to thermalize does not depend monotonically on the distance from equilibrium, allowing systems with an initially larger temperature difference to thermalize before those with a smaller one. The rarity of the effect makes it hard to find parameters that produce it, complicating the design of experiments and the development of applications. Here, we find necessary and sufficient conditions for the Markovian Mpemba effect in three-level systems and explain its underlying physical mechanisms. Based on our understanding of the three-level case, we develop an efficient algorithm to determine the parameters for the effect in $N$-level systems. This protocol could open the door for the realization of Mpemba experiments and related applications in large systems.

</details>


### [200] [Infinite ergodic theory and functional statistics of non-confined Feller process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10106)
*Vicenç Méndez*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究非束缚Feller过程的局部时场空间结构，证明长时间极限下局部时可因子化为非可归一化平稳密度刻画的空间剖面与单一Mittag-Leffler随机振幅，并给出占据时间与幂观测量的极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该过程具有非可归一化平稳密度、无限平均回返时间，属无限遍历理论范畴；此前工作多关注时间统计，本文转向其局部时场的空间结构，以期统一刻画状态依赖乘性噪声下的无限遍历现象。

Method: 对过程作确定性重标度后等价于平方Bessel过程；通过分析加性可观测量的长时间行为，推导局部时场的因子分解，并分别考察有限区间占据时间与非可积幂观测量两类泛函的极限分布。

Result: 局部时在长时间极限下可因子化：空间剖面由非可归一化平稳密度支配，时间涨落由单一Mittag-Leffler随机振幅控制。归一化空间相关收敛于与两个观测水平无关的普适常数。有限区间占据时间呈Darling-Kac涨落；非可积幂观测量则给出自相似平方Bessel型极限而非Mittag-Leffler统计。

Conclusion: 局部时空间场视角为状态依赖乘性噪声下的无限遍历理论提供了统一框架，区分了占据时间型与幂观测量型两类泛函的极限统计。

Abstract: We study additive observables of a non-confined Feller process characterized by a non- normalizable stationary probability density and return times with infinite mean. The process is equivalent, after a deterministic rescaling, to a squared Bessel process, but we focus here on a question: the spatial structure of its local-time field. We show that the local time admits a fac- torization in the long time limit. Its spatial profile is governed by the non-normalizable stationary density, whereas its temporal fluctuations are controlled by a single Mittag-Leffler random ampli- tude. As a consequence, normalized spatial correlations of the local time converge to a universal constant independent of the two observation levels. Occupation times of finite intervals follow as corollaries and display Darling-Kac fluctuations. In contrast, non-integrable power observables exhibit self-similar squared-Bessel-type limits rather than Mittag-Leffler statistics. This spatial- field perspective provides a unifying framework for infinite ergodic theory under state-dependent multiplicative noise.

</details>


### [201] [Anizotropic Ising Model on 2D Kagomé Lattice as an Inhomogeneous XYZ Integrable Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10163)
*Shahane A. Khachatryan,Hrachya Babujian,Ara G. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究kagomé晶格上具有交替耦合的广义非均匀二维Ising模型，将其映射到满足自由费米子条件的非对称八顶点R矩阵，分析Yang–Baxter结构并导出各向异性极限下的一维量子自旋链，同时给出部分各向异性模型的自由能与比热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望推广kagomé晶格上的非均匀二维Ising模型，利用可积性（Yang–Baxter结构与自由费米子条件）统一处理六个耦合参数，并揭示其对应的一维量子自旋链结构及热力学性质。

Method: 将局部Boltzmann权重映射为满足自由费米子条件的非对称八顶点R矩阵；针对$J'_i=J_i$和$J'_i=-J_i$两种情形分析Yang–Baxter结构，并在各向异性极限下导出相应的一维量子自旋链；对部分各向异性模型计算自由能和比热。

Result: $J'_i=J_i$情形给出横场Ising型哈密顿量；$J'_i=-J_i$情形给出带分级置换结构的修正链，并伴随配分函数零点移动；对部分各向异性模型获得了自由能和比热。

Conclusion: 该模型在任意六个耦合值下均可积，两类耦合关系分别对应不同的量子自旋链与零点结构，表明kagomé Ising模型的Yang–Baxter可积性可推广至更一般的非均匀与分级情形。

Abstract: We investigate a generalized inhomogeneous two-dimensional Ising model on the kagomé lattice with two alternating couplings $J_i$ and $J'_i$, $i=1,2,3$, along the three lattice directions. The local Boltzmann weights are mapped onto a non-symmetric eight-vertex $R$-matrix satisfying the free-fermion condition for arbitrary values of the six couplings. We analyze the Yang--Baxter structure for the cases $J'_i=J_i$ and $J'_i=-J_i$ and derive the corresponding one-dimensional quantum spin chains in the anisotropic limit. The first case yields a transverse-field Ising-type Hamiltonian, while the second leads to a modified chain with a graded permutation structure and shifted partition-function zeros. For a partially anisotropic model, we also obtain the free energy and specific heat.

</details>


### [202] [Italian Business-to-Business Invoicing Data: A Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10227)
*Valerio Astuti,Daniele Piras*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 基于意大利税务局全量电子发票数据，首次构建并刻画了意大利企业间商业关系网络，揭示其无标度结构、地理与行业分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业间（B2B）生产网络是理解经济系统结构与冲击传导的关键，但既往研究多依赖抽样或局部数据，缺乏覆盖全国、企业级粒度的完整商业关系刻画。本文旨在利用意大利税务局的电子发票全量数据，首次完整描述意大利生产系统的企业间网络。

Method: 利用意大利税务局收集的电子发票全量数据，构建企业与企业之间的商业交易网络；统计每家企业买方（客户）与卖方（供应商）数量的分布、各企业在生产网络中的中心性以及企业间平均距离，并给出网络的地理与行业分解，最后对各分布的尾部指数进行估计。

Result: 每家企业买方数与卖方数均服从重尾分布，表明网络具有无标度结构；中心性分布同样呈重尾，说明重要性高度集中于少数企业；所有分布的尾部指数估计显示，下游（买方侧）分布的指数普遍低于上游（卖方侧）。

Conclusion: 意大利B2B商业网络呈现显著的无标度与高度集中特征，买方侧与卖方侧在尾部行为上存在系统性差异，为后续研究生产网络上的冲击传导与政策分析提供了完整的实证基础。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive description of the network of Italian Business-to-Business commercial relationships, based on the universe of electronic invoices collected by the Italian Tax Office. The firm-to-firm detail of the data is exploited to describe the distribution of the numbers of buyers (customers) and sellers (suppliers) per firm, the centrality of each firm in the production network, and the average distance between firms. We characterize for the first time the firm-to-firm network describing the Italian production system, and present its geographic and sectoral breakdown. The analysis reveals heavy tailed distributions for the numbers of buyers and sellers per firm, implying a scale-free structure of the network. The distributions of centrality values display heavy tails as well, indicating a strong concentration of importance in a relatively small number of firms. We estimate the tail exponents for all these distributions, finding in all cases lower exponents for downstream (buyer-side) distributions than for the upstream (seller-side) ones.

</details>


### [203] [Quantifying translational and bond-orientational order metrics in hyperuniform and nonhyperuniform many-particle systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10359)
*Anirban Mukherjee,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入键取向序度量τ_O，与平移序度量τ_T构成二维统计对(τ_T,τ_O)，并系统计算了硬球平衡流体、RSA堆积及二维隐形超均匀基态三类模型中两种有序度随参数的变化，揭示了不同制备路径下的结构有序差异及两度量的正相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化多粒子体系中有序/无序程度是物理、材料科学与数学领域的长期难题。现有研究多聚焦于平移序的单一度量，缺乏将平移序与键取向序统一置于同一两点统计框架下的通用工具，难以刻画不同制备路径和不同维度下结构有序的差异。

Method: 定义平移序度量τ_T为总关联函数h(r)的L²范数平方；引入其键取向类似物τ_O，由加权总关联函数h_f(r)结合局域取向权重构造，形成统计对(τ_T,τ_O)。对三类模型进行计算：二维/三维平衡硬球流体、非平衡RSA堆积（以堆积分数φ为变量），以及二维无序隐形超均匀基态（隐形参数0<χ<1/2）。

Result: 在非超均匀体系中，键取向序始终处于次要地位，但在平衡流体分支上端其相对量显著增强（二维强于三维），在晶态分支上两度量趋于相当。相同堆积分数下，平衡流体与RSA堆积给出不同的(τ_T,τ_O)轨迹，显示制备依赖性。在二维隐形超均匀相中，键取向序仍为次要，但随接近无序-有序阈值相对量增大。三个模型中，超出泊松参考区后τ_T与τ_O正相关，τ_O随τ_T单调增加。

Conclusion: τ_T与τ_O构成的统计对为平移序与键取向序提供了统一的两点统计描述，能够区分不同制备路径与不同相区的结构有序特征，并揭示二维与三维、超均匀与非超均匀体系中取向序相对权重的系统规律。

Abstract: Quantifying the degree of order/disorder in many-particle systems remains an outstanding problem in physics, materials science, and mathematics. To this end, we consider the translational order metric $τ_T$, defined as the squared $L^2$ norm of the total correlation function $h(\mathbf{r})$, and introduce its bond-orientational analogue $τ_O$, defined from the weighted total correlation function $h_{\mathbf{f}}(\mathbf{r})$ using local orientational weights (S. Torquato et al., Phys. Rev. X 16, 011042 (2026)). The pair $(τ_T,τ_O)$ places both forms of order on a common two-point statistical footing. We compute both metrics for two nonhyperuniform sphere-packing models in 2D and 3D as functions of packing fraction $φ$: 1) equilibrium hard particles and 2) nonequilibrium random sequential addition (RSA) packings. For the nonhyperuniform systems, bond-orientational order remains subdominant along the equilibrium-fluid and RSA configurations; however, its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases near the upper end of the equilibrium-fluid branches, much more strongly in 2D than in 3D, and the two metrics become comparable along the sampled crystal branches. At common packing fractions, equilibrium fluids and RSA packings trace distinct $(τ_T,τ_O)$ trajectories, revealing preparation-dependent differences in structural order. As a representative hyperuniform family, we study 2D disordered stealthy hyperuniform (SHU) ground states for $0<χ<1/2$, where $χ$ is the stealthiness parameter. Within the disordered SHU phase, bond-orientational order remains subdominant, but its magnitude relative to the translational metric increases toward the disorder-to-order threshold. In all three models, $τ_T$ and $τ_O$ are positively correlated beyond the Poisson-reference regime: $τ_O$ increases monotonically with $τ_T$ across the sampled state points.

</details>


### [204] [Optimal Intermediate Hamiltonians for Non-Equilibrium Free Energy Calculations: A Numerical Study of Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10519)
*David Beyer,Helmut Grubmüller*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过倾斜主方程与自动微分优化中间哈密顿量，最小化Jarzynski估计量的均方误差，发现最优中间态在首末时刻跳跃，可将MSE降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jarzynski关系可从非平衡有限时间切换模拟估计平衡自由能差，但稀有轨迹主导指数功平均，导致收敛性差。作者希望找到连接初末态的中间哈密顿量序列，以最小化Jarzynski估计量的均方误差，从而改善收敛。

Method: 针对离散时间马尔可夫模型，推导大样本极限下MSE的精确倾斜主方程（tilted-master-equation）表示，结合自动微分，对所有中间能量进行高效的基于梯度的最小化。应用于三个复杂度递增的模型系统：两态模型、双势阱势和位移势阱。

Result: 三个系统中，最优中间哈密顿量均在初始和最终时刻发生跳跃。大量蒙特卡洛模拟表明，与线性及对数插值相比，最优中间态可将MSE降低超过一个数量级，在能量景观变化大时尤为显著。值得注意的是，最优中间态并不一定比误差更大的中间态耗散更少功。

Conclusion: 结果为现实分子体系的更高效非平衡自由能计算提供启发式规则：最优中间哈密顿量在首末时刻跳跃；对跨垒问题，势垒应快速降低后再升高；最小化耗散并不保证更快收敛。

Abstract: The Jarzynski relation enables the estimation of equilibrium free energy differences from non-equilibrium, finite-time switching simulations. These estimates usually converge poorly because rare trajectories dominate the exponential work average. Here, we numerically determined and explored the sequence of intermediate Hamiltonians connecting initial and final states that minimize the mean squared error (MSE) of the Jarzynski estimator and thereby enhance convergence. For discrete-time Markov models, an exact tilted-master-equation representation of the MSE in the large-sample limit, combined with automatic differentiation, enables efficient gradient-based minimization over all intermediate energies. We applied our approach to three model systems of increasing complexity: a two-state model, a double-well potential, and a shifted potential well. In all three systems, the optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations show that optimal intermediates can reduce the MSE by more than an order of magnitude compared with linear and logarithmic interpolation, most strongly for large changes in the energy landscape. Remarkably, they need not dissipate less work than intermediates yielding larger errors. Our results suggest heuristics for more efficient non-equilibrium free energy calculations of realistic molecular systems: optimal intermediate Hamiltonians jump at the initial and final times; for barrier-crossing problems, the barrier should be lowered rapidly and raised again later; and minimizing dissipation does not guarantee faster convergence.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Nonlinear flame describing function and mean shift kinematics of slit flames under combined axial-transverse forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09773)
*Juhoon Son,Yong Jea Kim,Jungho Sohn,Dong-hyuk Shin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于二维G方程水平集方法，揭示多向强迫下预混狭缝火焰的非线性运动学机制，证明非线性FDF行为本质上由运动学平均位移（mean shift）控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流火焰中几何非线性与其他热扩散、流体动力学不稳定性相互耦合，难以区分，缺乏解耦几何非线性的理论基准。本研究旨在厘清多维强迫下非线性火焰传递函数（FDF）背后的运动学机制。

Method: 采用二维G方程水平集框架模拟预混狭缝火焰的非线性运动学；通过多变量渐近展开在准稳态极限下解析量化平均位移，四阶项捕捉高振幅饱和机制；引入标度参数表征横向主导效应；用二阶低通滤波器构建统一理论模型；并进一步推导有限对流速度下的线性传递函数。

Result: 组合强迫诱导FDF非线性饱和，表现为增益提前平坦化和相位提前下降，随横向强迫幅值增强；几何非线性体现为时均火焰高度下降（平均位移）。压力极限下平均位移的频率依赖衰减在引入标度参数后坍缩为单一主曲线。用实测平均位移修正Strouhal数后，分散的非线性FDF曲线坍缩至线性理论预测。有限对流速度下修正仍能实现坍缩。

Conclusion: 多维强迫下非线性FDF行为从根本上由运动学平均位移支配，该结论为在湍流火焰中解耦几何非线性与其他热扩散或流体动力学不稳定性提供了理论基准。

Abstract: This study investigates the nonlinear kinematics of a premixed slit flame using a two-dimensional $G$-equation level-set framework. Results show that combined forcing induces nonlinear saturation in the FDF, characterized by early gain flattening and premature phase drops, which intensify with the transverse forcing amplitude. Kinematic analysis reveals that this geometric nonlinearity manifests as a reduction in the time-averaged flame height, defined as the mean shift. In the quasi-steady limit, this mean shift is analytically quantified via a multivariate asymptotic expansion, where fourth-order terms successfully capture the saturation mechanism at elevated amplitudes. By introducing a scaling parameter to account for transverse dominance, the frequency-dependent decay of the mean shift in the compact limit collapses onto a single master curve, enabling the derivation of a unified theoretical model that integrates this asymptotic response with a second-order low-pass filter. Furthermore, because the mean shift reduces the physical extent of the flame, it alters the wrinkle propagation time. Correcting the Strouhal number using the measured mean shift collapses the dispersed nonlinear FDF curves onto the linear theory prediction. The analysis is further extended to disturbances convected at a finite speed, for which the linear transfer function is derived analytically and the correction with the measured mean shift continues to collapse the nonlinear FDF. These findings establish that the nonlinear FDF behavior under multidimensional forcing is fundamentally governed by the kinematic mean shift, providing a theoretical baseline for decoupling geometric nonlinearities from other thermo-diffusive or hydrodynamic instabilities in turbulent flames.

</details>


### [206] [Signature of Short-Range Order in Static Response of the Three-Dimensional Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10006)
*Muhammed H. Güneş,Yubo Yang,Vitaly Gorelov,Miguel A. Morales,Matteo Gatti,Lucia Reining,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 利用扩散蒙特卡洛方法计算了三维电子气的静态局域场因子G(q)和静态密度-密度响应函数，发现在中等波矢处存在显著结构，并将其识别为短程有序的指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维电子气的交换关联能是材料从头算计算的起点，但其波矢相关的响应以及静态局域场因子G(q)在强耦合区域三十年来缺乏准确的基态基准。

Method: 使用扩散蒙特卡洛（Diffusion Monte Carlo）方法，在整个液相区域计算G(q)和静态密度-密度响应函数，并给出参数化。

Result: 在中等波矢处发现G(q)中存在显著结构，该结构在金属密度下即可见并随相互作用强度增强而增大；证明该结构是重现静态结构因子所必需的，因而将其识别为短程有序的指纹。参数化结果预测出粒子-空穴连续谱内存在一个低能共振模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的参数化在整个液相区域有效，为强耦合三维电子气的G(q)提供了准确的基态基准，并揭示了短程有序及低能共振模式。

Abstract: The three-dimensional electron gas is a fundamental model in condensed matter physics and quantum chemistry, and the exchange-correlation energy derived from it serves as the starting point of \textit{ab initio} computations of materials. However, the wave-vector-dependent response, and hence the static local field factor $G(q)$, has remained without accurate ground-state benchmark in the strongly coupled regime for three decades. Using diffusion Monte Carlo, we calculate $G(q)$ and the static density-density response function across the liquid phase and find a pronounced structure in $G(q)$ at intermediate wave vectors, already visible at metallic densities and growing with increasing interaction strength. We identify it as a fingerprint of short-range order by showing that it is required to reproduce the static structure factor. Our parametrization, valid in the entire liquid phase, predicts a low-energy resonant mode inside the particle-hole continuum.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [A Subsampled Davis-Kahan Bound for Large-Scale Eigenspace Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09211)
*Huan Qing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出独立伯努利采样方案，将Davis-Kahan定理扩展到子采样情形，给出显式误差界，揭示计算成本与采样概率线性、统计误差与采样概率平方根倒数之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Davis-Kahan定理是谱分析的基础工具，可定量控制对称矩阵特征子空间与其扰动之间的距离。但当矩阵维数很大时，计算前导特征向量代价高昂，限制了谱方法在大规模现代应用中的实用性。因此需要一种既能降低计算成本又能保持谱分析精度的方法。

Method: 提出独立伯努利采样方案；证明子采样矩阵的前导左奇异向量能够忠实逼近低秩对称矩阵的目标子空间；建立子采样Davis-Kahan界，给出直接依赖采样概率的显式误差界。

Result: 得到子采样Davis-Kahan界：该界揭示计算成本与采样概率呈线性关系，而统计误差与采样概率的平方根倒数成正比。

Conclusion: 该结果将Davis-Kahan定理推广到子采样情形，使大规模对称矩阵的可扩展谱分析成为可能。

Abstract: The Davis-Kahan theorem is a fundamental tool in spectral analysis, providing quantitative control over the distance between the eigenspaces of a symmetric matrix and its perturbation. However, when the matrix dimension is large, computing leading eigenvectors is computationally expensive, limiting the practical use of spectral methods in modern large-scale applications. This paper addresses this problem by proposing an independent Bernoulli sampling scheme and proves that the leading left singular vectors of the subsampled matrix faithfully approximate the target subspace of a low-rank symmetric matrix. Our main result is a subsampled Davis-Kahan bound that gives an explicit error bound depending directly on the sampling probability. The bound reveals the trade-off: the computational cost scales linearly with the sampling probability, while the statistical error scales as the inverse square root of the sampling probability. Our result thus extends the Davis-Kahan theorem to the subsampled setting, enabling scalable spectral analysis of large-scale symmetric matrices.

</details>


### [208] [Critical initialization destabilizes higher input derivatives in wide scalar-input networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09244)
*Prashant Singh,Pranav Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文在无限宽极限下推导了标量输入全连接网络前三阶导数的均值场递推，精确成立于方差不动点。临界初始化下，一阶导数方差与深度无关，而二阶导数方差在激活函数具有非零曲率时随深度线性增长；对分支尺度为L^{-1/2}的残差网络，证明任意固定有限阶导数的方差一致有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "混沌边缘"(edge-of-chaos)条件能保持宽随机初始化网络对一阶输入扰动的敏感性，但物理信息损失、score matching 和导数正则化等任务依赖更高阶输入导数。现有理论对高阶导数在临界初始化下的深度标度行为缺乏刻画，因此需要建立高阶导数的均值场理论。

Method: 针对光滑标量输入的全连接网络，利用在无限宽极限下、每个固定深度处成立的有限导数"jet"的联合高斯性，推导至三阶的均值场递推；该递推在方差不动点处精确，有限深度修正按几何速率衰减。临界点处分析各阶导数方差随深度的标度，并利用均值场磁化率使三阶系统闭合。对于分支尺度为L^{-1/2}的残差网络，在显式正则性假设下证明任意固定有限阶导数的方差一致有界。

Result: 在临界初始化下，一阶导数方差与深度无关（深度不变）；只要激活函数具有非零曲率，二阶导数方差随深度线性增长。三阶系统通过均值场磁化率闭合。残差网络（分支尺度L^{-1/2}）中每个固定有限阶导数的方差一致有界。仿真验证了临界增长规律、残差界以及闭合递推。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了宽随机初始化网络中高阶输入导数在临界初始化下的深度标度行为：一阶导数深度不变、二阶导数线性增长，而残差结构可保证任意有限阶导数方差有界。作者强调结论仅涉及初始化，不涉及训练后网络性能。

Abstract: The edge-of-chaos condition preserves first-order input perturbations in wide randomly initialized networks, but physics-informed losses, score matching and derivative regularization depend on higher input derivatives. For smooth scalar-input fully connected networks, using a joint Gaussianity of the finite derivative jet that holds in the infinite-width limit at each fixed depth, we derive mean-field recursions through third order that are exact at the variance fixed point, with finite-depth corrections that decay geometrically. At criticality, the first-derivative variance is depth-invariant, whereas the second-derivative variance grows linearly whenever the activation has nonzero curvature. The resulting third-order system closes on mean-field susceptibilities. For residual networks with branch scale L^{-1/2}, we prove that every fixed finite derivative order has uniformly bounded variance under explicit regularity assumptions. Simulations verify the critical growth laws, the residual bound, and the closed recursion. The results concern initialization, not trained-network performance.

</details>


### [209] [What Fixed-Rollout pass@k Evaluations Can Identify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09245)
*Pranav Singh,Prashant Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在固定每问题采样数 n、可交换任务的混合条件二项模型下，观测到的成功计数仅能辨识潜在每任务成功分布的 n 个矩，因此直接 pass@k 在 k≤n 时可辨识，而 k>n 的泛化外推 pass@k、尾部指数与尾部常数均不可辨识；作者给出保计数律构造、精确可辨识区间及实用置信证书与报告规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样评估中普遍将 pass@k 外推到远超每问题采样数 n 的范围（如 n=16 推 k=1000），但这一外推的统计正当性缺乏非参数层面的刻画。论文旨在说明固定深度计数实验究竟缺少哪些信息，并为参数化推理时扩展律提供非参数基线。

Method: 在合并/随机任务的 conditional-Binomial 模型中，以固定 n 的成功计数为观测，分析其可辨识性：证明仅 n 个自由矩被辨识；构造保计数律但外推结果不相容的精确反例；指出唯一延拓的例外情形；利用 Hausdorff 主表示计算尖锐的总体可辨识区间。并在 Brown 等公开的每问题 10,000 rollout 数据上做反事实 n=16 的评估。

Result: 直接 pass@k（k≤n）可辨识，但 k>n 的泛化外推、尾部指数与尾部常数不可辨识，即使任务数任意多亦然。在 10,000 rollout 数据上，反事实 n=16 时 k=1000 的失败率在四种 MATH/GSM8K/CodeContests 配置下存在 1.5 倍至 2600 倍以上的歧义；校准显示中等规模失败份额本身不能决定区间宽度。

Conclusion: 该结果不否定参数化推理时扩展律，而是提供评估其假设的非参数基线。作者给出精确、保守的单坐标有限任务置信证书，以及区分直接估计、可辨识集合与模型条件预测的报告标准。

Abstract: Repeated-sampling evaluations increasingly extrapolate pass@k far beyond the number n of samples collected per problem. We show that, in the pooled/random-task conditional-Binomial model, fixed-n success counts identify only the n free moments of the latent per-task success distribution. Consequently, direct pass@k is identified for k <= n, but generic extrapolated pass@k, tail exponents, and tail constants are not identified for k > n, even with arbitrarily many exchangeable tasks at the same rollout budget. This is stronger than the observation that the usual estimator is undefined beyond n: it characterizes the information missing from the fixed-depth count-law experiment. We give exact count-law-preserving constructions with incompatible extrapolations, state the exceptional unique-extension case, and compute sharp population identified intervals through Hausdorff principal representations. On the public 10,000-rollout-per-problem release of Brown et al., counterfactual n = 16 evaluations leave failure at k = 1000 ambiguous by factors from 1.5 to over 2,600 across four MATH/GSM8K/CodeContests configurations. The calibration shows that intermediate-scale failure share alone does not determine width. Our result does not reject parametric inference-time scaling laws; it supplies the nonparametric baseline against which their assumptions can be evaluated. We give an exact, conservative one-coordinate finite-task confidence certificate and a reporting standard separating direct estimates, identified sets, and model-conditioned forecasts.

</details>


### [210] [CAST: Canonical Approximate Schur Tree for Approximate Cholesky on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09255)
*Meher Chaitanya,Cameron Musco,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出CAST（规范近似Schur树）及其推广CAST-ρ，用从枢轴邻居团中采样的加权随机生成树替代稠密Schur补团，从而构造无偏且连通的近似Cholesky预条件子更新，并在图数据工作负载中取得效率与精度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图数据工作负载（扩散估计、排序、半监督学习、网络优化）常需对同一系数矩阵求解大量Laplacian或SDDM系统，近似Cholesky预条件子的构造成本可通过多次求解摊销。但消去枢轴顶点时会在活跃邻居间产生稠密Schur补团，导致填充和构造开销增大，因此需要一种更稀疏、无偏且可控的替代更新方式。

Method: 提出CAST：用直接从Schur补团中采样的加权随机生成树替代稠密团，每次实现连通且恰含d-1条边，通过对选中边按树包含概率取倒数重新加权保证更新无偏；分布与枢轴邻居顺序无关，并证明其杠杆分数边缘分布在无偏逆边缘单树估计量中最小化最大归一化重加权边贡献。进一步提出CAST-ρ：将每个邻居复制ρ份、每份携带1/ρ的关联权重，在扩展团上采加权随机生成树后再收缩回原邻域，更新仍无偏且连通，可在O(ρd)时间内精确采样，并满足归一化局部Schur误差二阶矩的1/ρ界。

Result: 理论上，CAST的分布具有顺序无关性，且其杠杆分数边缘分布在最优性意义上最小化最大归一化重加权边贡献；CAST-ρ保持无偏与连通，可O(ρd)精确采样，局部Schur误差二阶矩有1/ρ界，增大ρ可降低经验证的局部采样方差，但可能增加构造成本与下游填充。实验观察到CAST-1是更快的默认选择，而当额外边贡献仍较廉价时CAST-2更优。

Conclusion: CAST以加权随机生成树替代稠密Schur补团，在保证无偏性和连通性的同时降低填充与构造开销；CAST-ρ通过复制-收缩机制提供了方差与成本的可调权衡。实践中推荐CAST-1作为快速默认，CAST-2在额外边代价低廉时作为更优选择。

Abstract: Graph-data workloads such as diffusion estimation, ranking, semi-supervised learning, and network optimization often solve many Laplacian or symmetric diagonally dominant M-matrix (SDDM) systems with the same coefficient matrix. Approximate Cholesky preconditioners eliminate vertices one at a time and store the resulting sparse approximate factorization, the \emph{factor}, whose construction cost is amortized across these solves. But eliminating a vertex, the \emph{pivot}, creates a dense Schur-complement clique among its $d$ active neighbors. We introduce CAST (Canonical Approximate Schur Tree), which replaces this clique with a weighted random spanning tree sampled directly from it. Every realization is connected and contains exactly d-1 edges, while reweighting each selected edge by the reciprocal of its tree-inclusion probability makes the update unbiased. The distribution is independent of the ordering of the pivot neighbors, and we prove that its leverage-score marginals minimize the largest normalized reweighted-edge contribution among unbiased inverse-marginal one-tree estimators. We also introduce CAST-$ρ$, which replaces each pivot neighbor with $ρ$ copies, each carrying a $1/ρ$ share of that neighbor's incident weight, samples a weighted random spanning tree on the expanded clique, and contracts the copies back to the original neighborhood. The resulting update remains unbiased and connected, can be sampled exactly in $O(ρd)$ time, and satisfies a $1/ρ$ bound on the second moment of the normalized local Schur error. Increasing $ρ$ therefore reduces certified local sampling variability, but may increase construction cost and downstream fill. Empirically, we observe that CAST-1 is the faster default, whereas CAST-2 is preferable when its additional edge contributions remain inexpensive.

</details>


### [211] [Tensor Network Moral Graph Recovery of Discrete Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09258)
*Á. Troyano Olivas,Chi-Hang Fred Fung,Hans H. Brunner,Momtchil Peev,Vicente Martin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出用全连接张量网络(FCTN)配合核范数正则化的键修正，从离散变量的概率分布中恢复因果DAG的道德图；在忠实性、正性与无隐式重路由假设下，证明零重构误差时最优FCTN的有效图恰为道德图，并给出近似情形下的恢复界与正则参数充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从概率分布恢复因果结构（尤其是道德图/骨架）是因果发现的核心问题。传统方法多依赖条件独立性检验，其统计与泛化性质不易刻画。作者希望借助张量网络的可解释键结构，构造一个有理论保证的结构恢复框架，把「有效图」直接编码在键矩阵中，从而在精确与近似两种情形下都能给出严格结论。

Method: 采用全连接张量网络(FCTN)表示离散变量分布。每个键矩阵参数化为全1基线矩阵加低秩修正 C_ij = U_ij V_ij^T，并对修正施加核范数正则（通过因子的变分Frobenius范数惩罚实现），以驱动多余键趋于零。有效图直接从优化后的键矩阵读出。理论分析基于忠实性、正性以及局部张量架构上的无隐式重路由假设；近似情形利用条件互信息的Fannes-Audenaert连续性给出显式恢复界，并推导正则参数β的充分条件。

Result: 在忠实性、正性与无隐式重路由假设下，证明每个重构误差 ε = 0 的最优FCTN其有效图恰等于真实道德图。对近似情形 ε > 0，给出基于条件互信息Fannes-Audenaert连续性的显式恢复界，并得到正则参数β的一个充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作把道德图恢复转化为带低秩核范数正则的FCTN优化问题，为精确与近似两种情形提供了结构恢复的理论保证，并表明有效图可直接从键矩阵读取，为基于张量网络的因果结构学习提供了新的理论与方法基础。

Abstract: We present a method for recovering the moral graph of a causal DAG from a probability distribution over discrete variables, using fully connected tensor networks (FCTNs) with nuclear-norm-regularized bond corrections. Each bond matrix is parameterized as a baseline all-ones matrix plus a low-rank correction $C_{ij} = U_{ij}V_{ij}^\top$, and the nuclear norm of the correction implemented via the variational Frobenius norm penalty on the factors drives unnecessary bonds to zero. We prove that under faithfulness, positivity, and a no-implicit-rerouting assumption on the local tensor architecture, \textbf{every} optimal FCTN with zero reconstruction error $\varepsilon = 0$ has effective graph exactly equal to the moral graph. For the approximate regime ($\varepsilon > 0$), we provide explicit recovery bounds using the Fannes-Audenaert continuity of conditional mutual information, and derive a sufficient condition on the regularization parameter $β$. The effective graph is read directly from the optimized bond matrices.

</details>


### [212] [Mode Coverage in Normalizing Flow Boltzmann Generators via Log-Ratio Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09473)
*Qi Feng,Rongjie Lai,Di Qi,Xuda Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出log-ratio variation X_ω与KLXX损失，增强归一化流Boltzmann生成器的模式覆盖与采样可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正向KL训练依赖可能有偏或缺失模式的目标样本，导致流可能遗漏目标质量，而重要性权重给出的有效样本量仍偏高，从而掩盖问题。

Method: 定义目标到推前对数密度比在加权测度ω下的平均绝对成对差X_ω，并构造KLXX损失：在正向KL上加入两个log-ratio variation（一个用目标加权提升精度，另一个用quench/temper样本与推前样本的混合搜索候选模式）。推导KLXX的Fisher–Rao梯度流（两项均贡献非正耗散）及固定代理误差界；将其用于自适应分阶段Boltzmann生成器并在每阶段做重要性重加权，给出阶段权重本质有界时的推断误差界与随样本量渐近无偏性。

Result: 数值测试中KLXX相较正向KL提升模式覆盖，改进生成器各阶段诊断（相对构建调度所用损失），恢复的可观测量接近独立参考。

Conclusion: log-ratio variation提供了正向KL通常遗漏的信息，可缓解模式漏失与诊断失真问题。

Abstract: Normalizing flow Boltzmann generators retain a tractable pushforward density, but training with forward KL depends on target samples that may be biased or omit modes. As a result, a flow can miss target mass while its observed importance weights give a high effective sample size. We introduce the log-ratio variation $\X_ω$, the mean absolute pairwise difference of the target-to-pushforward log-density ratio under a weighting measure $ω$, and use it to define KLXX, a new loss function. Two log-ratio variations are added to the forward KL (denoted by the two X's): one weighted by the target to improve accuracy, the other by a mixture of quench and temper samples with pushforward samples to search candidate modes. We derive the Fisher--Rao gradient flow of KLXX, where both variations contribute nonpositive dissipation, and a fixed-surrogate error bound for KLXX. We use KLXX in an adaptive-staging Boltzmann generator, with importance reweighting at every stage. We bound the sampling error of its inference scheme when the stage weights are essentially bounded, and prove it asymptotically unbiased in the sample size. In the numerical tests, KLXX improves mode coverage over forward KL. It also improves the generator's per-stage diagnostics against the loss that built the schedule. The observables the generator recovers are close to independent references. The log-ratio variations thus supply information that the forward KL loss usually omits.

</details>


### [213] [Recovery Theory for Projected Power Iterations in Permutation Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09502)
*Vahan Huroyan,Gilad Lerman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究投影幂法（PPM）在稀疏均匀噪声模型下同步n个未知置换问题，证明了单步精确恢复、块误差收缩、轨迹稳定性及端到端近精确恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换同步是诸多机器学习与信号处理任务的核心问题，现有理论对稀疏观测与对抗性均匀置换噪声下的投影幂法收敛性、误差收缩率与恢复精度缺乏深入刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用投影幂法（PPM）求解置换同步问题。在每对以概率p观测、观测以概率π₀未受污染否则为独立均匀置换的模型下，结合参考块谱初始化方法；通过分析最优对齐块误差，建立单步收缩与迭代轨迹性质，并将结果推广到独立非同分布置换值噪声、部分置换及变支撑情形。

Result: 在log m=o(npπ₀²)条件下证明对固定正多数正确块的独立估计可一步精确恢复；在np≥C₀ log n且m=o(npπ₀²)时，对最优对齐误差≤0.5-ε的任意估计建立块误差收缩，收缩因子为O(m/(npπ₀²))，误差底为O(e^{-cnpπ₀}+e^{-cnpπ₀²}+log n/n)；参考块谱初始化达到对齐块误差O_P(m/(npπ₀²))，实现端到端近精确恢复；在更强全块信号条件下PPM有限步达精确恢复；理论可精确迁移到共支撑部分置换，对变支撑给出确定性及概率共可见性裕度。

Conclusion: 投影幂法在稀疏均匀噪声置换同步中具备强收敛与恢复保证：单步收缩与轨迹稳定性使任意数据依赖估计在盆内映射到消失块误差且后续迭代几乎精确；理论可推广至非均匀噪声与部分置换情形，为置换同步提供统一分析框架。

Abstract: We study the projected power method (PPM) for synchronizing \(n\) unknown permutations of \(m\) objects under a possibly sparse uniform corruption model. Each pair is observed with probability \(p\), and an observed measurement is uncorrupted with probability \(π_0\) and is otherwise an independent uniform permutation. Under \(\log m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove exact one-step recovery (with high probability) of each prescribed block for an independent estimate with a fixed positive majority of correct blocks. When \(np\ge C_0\log n\) and \(m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove that one high-probability event yields a block-error contraction simultaneously for every estimate whose optimally aligned error is at most \(0.5-ε\). The contraction factor is \(O(m/(npπ_0^2))\) and the error floor is \(O(e^{-cnpπ_0}+e^{-cnpπ_0^2}+{\log n}/{n})\). Consequently, one update maps every possibly data-dependent estimate in this basin to vanishing block error, and all subsequent iterates remain almost exact uniformly over the iteration index. The one-step and trajectory results extend to independent, non-identically distributed, permutation-valued corruptions with mean \(m^{-1} \mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\). Under the uniform model, a reference-block spectral initializer has aligned block error \(O_{\mathbb P}(m/(npπ_0^2))\), yielding an end-to-end almost-exact recovery guarantee. Under a stronger all-block signal condition, PPM reaches exact recovery after finitely many iterations. The theory transfers exactly to partial permutations with common support; for varying supports, we establish deterministic and probabilistic co-visibility margins.

</details>


### [214] [High-probability guarantees for linear accessibility in feature superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09556)
*Enrico Vompa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将线性可及性问题建模为压缩感知问题，证明在次高斯噪声下固定支撑的充分维度为线性规模，而非以往的最坏情况二次规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可通过特征叠加编码比维度更多的概念，但跨特征干扰限制了同时激活特征的线性可及性，缺乏对所需维度的精确理论刻画。

Method: 将线性可及性建模为压缩感知问题，在次高斯噪声下推导固定支撑的高概率界，并通过高斯尾部近似对系统参数进行验证。

Result: 得到充分维度随概念数与稀疏度呈线性尺度（d=O_ε(k log m)），优于此前的最坏情况二次上界，并在不同系统参数下验证了该界。

Conclusion: 量化了线性表示假设的几何约束，为评估稀疏自编码器、组合泛化与神经可解释性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Neural networks can leverage feature superposition to encode more concepts than dimensions, but cross-feature interference constrains the linear accessibility of simultaneously active features. By framing linear accessibility as a compressed sensing problem, we derive high-probability bounds for fixed supports under subgaussian noise, proving the sufficient dimension scales linearly ($d=O_{\varepsilon}(k \log m)$) rather than prior worst-case quadratic limits. We then validate these bounds across system parameters through Gaussian-tail approximations. These results quantify the geometric constraints of the linear representation hypothesis, providing a framework for evaluating sparse autoencoders, compositional generalization, and neural interpretability.

</details>


### [215] [Learning with Synthetic Data via SGD in High-Dimensional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09572)
*Jichu li,Difan Zou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据对高维线性回归中单遍SGD泛化的影响，建立混合训练与两阶段训练的有限样本风险界，揭示混合训练导致强模型坍缩而两阶段训练可避免坍缩，并给出缩放律与两阶段训练优于纯真实数据训练的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据被用于突破人类数据规模限制以扩展模型训练，但可能引发强模型坍缩：即使固定比例的合成数据也会阻止模型性能随数据规模提升，留下无法消失的额外风险下界。因此需要理论刻画合成数据在何种训练协议下、以何种方式影响泛化。

Method: 以带模型偏移的高维线性回归为设定，研究单遍SGD的泛化。分别针对混合训练与两阶段训练建立有限样本风险界，将标准偏差与方差项同源不匹配效应分离（混合下的波动与持续漂移、两阶段下的过滤初始化偏差）。在随机素描模型下推导两种协议的缩放律，并针对混合训练在优化饱和区给出紧致结果；最后推导两阶段训练在相同真实数据预算与相同真实阶段更新下严格优于纯真实数据训练的有限样本充要条件。

Result: 风险界显示鲜明对比：混合训练引发强模型坍缩，而两阶段训练因仅在第一阶段使用合成数据而避免风险下界，说明坍缩在简单数据课程下并非必然。随机素描模型下的缩放律表明，混合训练中更大的模型可能放大合成数据导致的退化；两阶段训练中高质量合成预训练可降低偏差。此外获得两阶段训练严格优于纯真实数据训练的精确有限样本充要条件。

Conclusion: 合成数据既非固有有害也非固有有益，其效果关键取决于合成数据质量与将其纳入训练所用的协议；两阶段数据课程是一种能规避模型坍缩的有效方案。

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a promising way to scale model training beyond limited human-generated data but it may also induce strong model collapse (Dohmatob et al., 2024), where any fixed fraction of synthetic data prevents model performance from improving under data scaling, leaving a non-vanishing excess risk floor. In this paper, we study how synthetic data affects the generalization of one-pass SGD in high-dimensional linear regression with model shift. We establish finite-sample risk bounds for mixed and two-stage training, separating standard bias and variance from source-mismatch effects, namely fluctuation and persistent drift under mixing and filtered initialization bias under two-stage. These bounds reveal a sharp contrast: mixed training induces strong model collapse, while two-stage training avoids the floor by using synthetic data only in the first stage, showing that collapse is not inevitable under a simple data curriculum. Under a random sketch model, we further obtain scaling laws for both protocols, with tight results for mixed training in the optimization-saturated regime. These laws show that larger models may amplify synthetic-induced degradation under mixing, and quantify how high-quality synthetic pretraining may reduce bias in two-stage training. Finally, we establish an exact finite-sample necessary-and-sufficient condition for two-stage training to strictly outperform real-only training under the same real-data budget and identical real-stage updates. Overall, our results highlight that synthetic data is neither inherently harmful nor beneficial; its effect depends critically on both its quality and the training protocol used to incorporate it.

</details>


### [216] [Distillation of Synthetic Data for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09586)
*Niloy Biswas,Noureddine El Karoui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出合成数据蒸馏（SDD），用时序轨迹的条件预测分布替代实际观测值作为损失目标，可视为训练目标的Rao-Blackwell化，在保持随机梯度期望不变的同时降低其协方差。在4M至2.5B参数的TSFM上验证，各规模收敛更快，Gaussian Process数据上达到或优于原损失且训练迭代减少10%–40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM预训练在合成时序轨迹上进行，数据生成过程已知，但损失目标仅比较模型输出与各轨迹的已实现未来值，忽略了生成过程提供的分布信息，导致梯度方差较大、训练效率低。

Method: 提出合成数据蒸馏（SDD）：将损失目标由与已实现未来值的比较改为与每条轨迹的条件预测分布比较。理论上证明SDD是训练目标的Rao-Blackwell化，随机梯度期望不变，且在Loewner偏序下协方差可证降低。在4M至2.5B参数规模的TSFM模型族上进行实证验证。

Result: 在所有模型规模上观察到验证损失收敛更快；在Gaussian Process数据上，SDD达到或优于原有损失（Status Quo loss），同时所需训练迭代减少10%–40%。

Conclusion: 利用合成数据已知的生成过程，以条件预测分布为损失目标可降低梯度方差、加速收敛，且不改变梯度期望，是一种可扩展、模型规模无关的有效预训练改进。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pre-trained on synthetically generated time series trajectories, where the data generating process is known. Current pre-training recipes are based on loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to realized future values of each trajectory. We instead propose loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to the conditional forecast distribution of each trajectory, a procedure we call synthetic data distillation (SDD). SDD corresponds to a Rao-Blackwellization of the training objective, in that it leaves the expectation of stochastic gradients unchanged while provably reducing the covariance of the stochastic gradient under the Loewner partial ordering. We empirically validate SDD on a TSFM model family of sizes from $4$M to $2.5$B parameters, and observe faster convergence of validation loss at every model size: on Gaussian Process data, SDD attains or improves upon the Status Quo loss whilst requiring $10\%-40\%$ less training iterations.

</details>


### [217] [Why Learning Rediscovers the Closed-Form Diagonal Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09656)
*Jeahn Han,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出模态逆问题中的"对角饱和原理"：在各向同性截断噪声下，Bayes最优的Tikhonov形状是一个仅由先验决定的闭式幂律Gamma_k ∝ lambda_k^|s|，与域无关；Berry随机波猜想与Weyl特征值计数律共同保证该结论在经验性Berry违背下依然成立，预测逐模态族上的损失地形近似平坦，因而对角正则化器难以稳健超越闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于厘清模态逆问题中"学习型正则化"究竟能带来多少实质收益：即对角正则化器是否存在一个理论上的性能上限，使得其相对解析闭式解的优势被限制在狭窄范围内，从而界定"学习何时才开始有用"的边界。

Method: 方法上，先刻画截断噪声各向同性条件下Bayes最优Tikhonov形状的闭式幂律；借助Berry随机波猜想论证截断噪声在模态间的去相关，并用Weyl特征值计数律保证模态数目足以使结论在经验性Berry违背下仍成立；随后在FEM仿真的声学房间上，将闭式解与逐房间Oracle调参及三种对角架构（在相同数据上训练）进行对比；并通过已知的指数Green函数修正，将框架无新增自由参数地推广到热扩散问题。

Result: 结果显示：在FEM仿真声学房间中，闭式解相对逐房间Oracle调参接近最优；三种对角架构虽学到性质不同的谱，其重建误差与闭式解的差距在1个百分点以内。饱和性仅限于对角族：Learned Iterative Ridge通过利用跨模态耦合越过了该边界，从而定位出学习开始起作用的区域。

Conclusion: 结论是：对角正则化存在饱和原理，其性能被仅由先验决定的闭式幂律所界定，学习在对角族内难以稳健超越该闭式解；只有当模型突破对角限制、利用跨模态耦合时，学习才真正产生增益。

Abstract: We identify a diagonal saturation principle in modal inverse problems: when truncation noise is isotropic, the Bayes-optimal Tikhonov shape is a closed-form power law Gamma_k proportional to lambda_k^|s| set by the prior alone, independent of the domain. Berry's random-wave conjecture decorrelates the truncation noise across modes, and Weyl's eigenvalue counting law supplies enough modes for the conclusion to survive empirical Berry violations. Together they predict an approximately flat loss landscape across the per-mode family, leaving narrow scope for a diagonal regularizer to robustly beat the closed form. On FEM-simulated acoustic rooms, the closed form is near-optimal relative to per-room oracle tuning across observation windows, and three diagonal architectures trained on the same data match its reconstruction error within 1 pp despite learning qualitatively different spectra. The framework extends to heat diffusion via a known exponential Green's function correction with no new free parameters. Saturation is restricted to the diagonal family: Learned Iterative Ridge crosses the boundary by exploiting cross-mode coupling, locating where learning starts to help.

</details>


### [218] [A Unifying Perspective on Probabilities as Model Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09855)
*Benedikt Höltgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"预测方法"框架，将概率统一理解为抽象构建与预测转换的产物，论证其模型依赖性，并基于有限校准准则说明概率如何支撑有限事件集上的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率陈述虽普遍存在，但其理解仍存根本分歧（如贝叶斯派与频率派之争），且何时、为何依据概率行动能带来理想结果尚不明确，需要统一视角。

Method: 将每个概率视为一种"预测方法"的输出，即由特定抽象构建方式及其向预测的转换方式共同决定；并引入有限校准准则，结合归纳论证与概率演算展开分析。

Result: 表明看似不同的概率类型可被统一，甚至所谓客观概率也依赖模型；当满足有限校准准则时，可预期给定策略的效用分布，并在有限事件集上指导成功决策，且该准则在许多情形下可行。

Conclusion: 构建了关于概率及其使用的连贯视角，整合了其他解释背后的关键直觉，为概率理解与决策应用提供统一框架。

Abstract: Although probabilistic statements are ubiquitous, foundational disagreements persist about their understanding, as exemplified by debates between Bayesians and frequentists; moreover, it is unclear when and why acting on them actually leads to desirable outcomes. Here, we argue that every probability is the output of a \emph{prediction method}, that is, it depends on both a particular way of constructing abstractions and a way of transforming them into predictions. Through this, we provide a unifying perspective on supposedly different kinds of probabilities and show that even supposedly objective ones are model-dependent. We demonstrate that when a finite calibration criterion is met, one can anticipate the distribution of utilities for a given policy and inform successful decision-making on finite sets of events. Based on the notion of prediction methods, inductive arguments, and the probability calculus, we explain the feasibility of the calibration criterion in many settings. Overall, we develop a coherent perspective on probabilities and their use, connecting key intuitions behind other interpretations along the way.

</details>


### [219] [FlowCPO: A Unified Divergence View of Preference Alignment for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09905)
*Yansen Han,Shengyi Liao,Peng Sun,Deyuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Tao Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过散度视角统一流/扩散模型的偏好对齐方法，提出离线前向KL目标FlowCPO，并给出可解对比式替代损失，实验显示域内指标优于FlowDPO，域外表现参差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配与扩散模型的偏好对齐分为在线强化学习和离线偏好优化两类，但二者关系不清晰：在线前向过程方法需从当前模型重新采样，离线方法多依赖正样本微调或DPO式似然比替代目标，缺乏统一理解且离线方案利用信息不足。

Method: 以散度为基础组织现有对齐方法，提出FlowCPO——一种离线前向KL目标，同时使用偏好与非偏好样本且无需在线采样。针对线性插值，在显式正则条件下证明前向KL目标被对比式流匹配损失上界约束，从而在固定数据上得到可解替代损失；并分析该损失非负，而简化FlowDPO的有符号回归损失可无下界。

Result: 域内设置下，CFG 3.0时FlowCPO平均GenEval与OCR得分分别为0.84和0.87，高于FlowDPO的0.81和0.74。域外设置结果混合：GenEval取得最佳，但在多项指标上奖励得分低于RFT。

Conclusion: FlowCPO为离线流/扩散偏好对齐提供了散度视角下的统一框架与理论有界的替代目标，域内显著提升生成与文字渲染质量，但域外泛化仍需改进。

Abstract: Preference alignment for flow and diffusion models now spans online reinforcement learning and offline preference optimization, but the relation between these methods remains unclear. In particular, existing forward-process alignment methods require fresh samples from the current model, while offline methods based on fixed preference pairs rely primarily on positive-only fine-tuning or DPO-style likelihood-ratio surrogates. We organize these approaches through a divergence-based framework and introduce FlowCPO, an offline forward-KL objective that uses both preferred and dispreferred samples without online rollouts. For linear interpolation, we show under explicit regularity conditions that the forward-KL objective is bounded by a contrastive flow matching loss, yielding a tractable surrogate on fixed data. We further show that this loss is nonnegative, whereas the signed regression loss of simplified FlowDPO can be unbounded below. In the in-domain setting, FlowCPO achieves higher mean GenEval and OCR scores than the evaluated baselines, reaching 0.84 and 0.87 versus 0.81 and 0.74 for FlowDPO at CFG 3.0. In the out-of-domain setting, the results are mixed, with the best GenEval result but lower reward scores than RFT on several metrics.

</details>


### [220] [Optimal Value Inference for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09981)
*Nan Lu,Ethan Lee,James M. Robins,David Simchi-Levi,Junwei Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究强化学习中最优价值的离线推断问题。作者通过对最大贝尔曼算子做 softmax 近似，将两个新的滋扰参数刻画为自诱导贝尔曼方程的固定点，进而提出基于 Neyman 正交性的去偏估计量，并在发散时间horizon下建立渐近正态性，最后通过合成实验与真实决策任务验证方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线强化学习中，对最优价值进行统计推断十分困难：最大贝尔曼算子不可微、行为策略可能随时间变化，且价值估计往往因过估计偏差与nuisances估计误差而难以获得有效置信区间。论文旨在为最优价值提供具有渐近正态性的有效推断方法。

Method: 1) 用 softmax 对应形式近似最大贝尔曼算子；2) 将两个新滋扰参数定义为自诱导贝尔曼方程的固定点；3) 基于 Neyman 正交性构造去偏估计量以减弱nuisances估计误差的一阶影响；4) 在允许行为策略随时间变化、时间horizon发散的情形下建立渐近正态性；5) 给出nuisances的具体估计流程，说明常见机器学习方法可达到所需统计速率。

Result: 理论上，估计量在发散horizon下具有渐近正态性，且对行为策略随时间变化稳健，仅要求nuisances达到可由多种机器学习方法实现的统计速率；实证上，合成实验验证了推断方法的数值表现，并在自行车调度与AI智能体工具使用等真实决策问题中成功实施。

Conclusion: 通过 softmax 近似最大贝尔曼算子并利用自诱导贝尔曼方程的固定点刻画nuisances，可以有效实现强化学习最优价值的去偏离线推断；该方法兼具理论保证与实用价值，可应用于实际决策场景。

Abstract: We study offline inference for the optimal value in reinforcement learning. Two new nuisances are derived as fixed points of a self-induced Bellman equation, in which we approximate the maximum Bellman operator by its softmax correspondence. We propose a debiased estimator through the Neyman orthogonality and establish its asymptotic normality under diverging horizons even when the behavior policy changes with time, as long as the nuisances have the statistical rates that can be achieved by many machine learning methods. We provide a concrete estimating procedure for these nuisances and show they can lead to valid inference. Synthetic experiments validate the numerical performance of our inference method, and we implement it in real-life decision-making problems, including bike repositioning and AI agentic tool use.

</details>


### [221] [A statistical approach to bias in zero-shot learning: the lens of handwriting recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10084)
*Clarence Chew,Gim Siang Chia,Sukalpa Chanda,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Soumendu Sundar Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对广义零样本学习(GZSL)中大规模未见类别的误分类偏置问题，以超大规模词汇的零样本手写词识别为背景，提出一种统计偏置矫正方法：将任意经典GZSL学习器视为黑盒，用两阶段层次架构（黑盒+轻量蒙特卡洛纠偏器集成）先预测样本的训练状态（已见/未见），再在预测状态内做分类，在大规模未见词识别上取得超过20%的相对精度提升，并发现约15维低维表征即可支撑大词汇词识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GZSL方法适用范围受限于较少数量的未见类，且因对训练中已见类的误分类偏置，难以扩展到大规模未见类。作者希望借助超大规模词汇的零样本手写词识别这一场景，研究并缓解GZSL的可扩展性与偏置问题。

Method: 提出统计式偏置矫正方法，将经典GZSL特征学习器当作黑盒处理，把判断数据点训练状态（已见vs未见）的内在偏置类比为分布外推断问题。采用两阶段层次架构：第一阶段为经典GZSL黑盒，第二阶段为轻量蒙特卡洛偏置矫正器集成。去偏后，测试数据分类仅在其预测的训练状态内进行，并结合最近邻、逻辑回归、随机森林等统计方法。方法配有数学分析支撑，且可与任意经典GZSL学习器即插即用式结合。

Result: 相比已有技术，未见词分类的相对精度提升超过20%；同时发现大规模词汇的词识别可采用约15维的低维表征。

Conclusion: 该统计偏置矫正方法能有效缓解GZSL对已见类的偏置，显著提升大规模未见词的识别精度，并支持低维表征。由于其以黑盒方式与任意经典GZSL学习器结合，具有广泛的适用性和跨领域推广潜力。

Abstract: Generalized zero-shot learning (GZSL) has emerged as an important paradigm for visual recognition systems that must generalize to classes that were not observed during training. Traditional GZSL techniques are limited by their applicability to a relatively small number of such unseen classes, scalability beyond which is challenging due to its well-known misclassification bias towards classes observed during training. In this work, we investigate the GZSL paradigm through the lens of zero-shot handwritten word recognition over extremely large vocabularies. We propose a statistical approach to rectifying this bias, which views any classical GZSL feature learner as a black box mechanism whose intrinsic bias in identifying the training status (seen vs. unseen) of a typical data point we aim to correct, similar to an out of distribution inferential problem. Our method leverages a simple two-stage hierarchical architecture, combining a classical GZSL blackbox in the first stage and an ensemble of lightweight Monte Carlo bias-correctors in the second. Once debiased, the classification of test data is undertaken only restricted to its predicted training status via well-founded statistical methods (eg nearest neighbour, logistic regression and random forests). We achieve relative accuracy improvements of over 20% in the classification of unseen words compared to established techniques. A key outcome is that word recognition over large scale vocabularies is amenable to a much lower dimensional representation (~15 dimensions). Our approach is underpinned by mathematical analysis that captures the essence of the statistical approach to bias correction. Our approach to bias rectification can be combined in a turn-key fashion with any classical GZSL learner as a blackbox, thereby suggesting a wide scope of applicability of this method for a wide variety of GZSL implementations in different domains.

</details>
